Pawan Jury BhilwaraRj

Mon Jul 11 2016 17:28:54 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

पाकिस्तान की सीमा पर सोनिया-मोदी-केजरीवाल दीवार बना रहे है --- अमेरिका और मेक्सिको की सीमा पर न तो कोई दीवार है और न ही कोई बाड़। तब अमेरिका मेक्सिको की सीमा से आतंकियों को घुसने से कैसे रोकता है ?
.
और अमेरिका को मेक्सिको-अमेरिका के बीच बिछी सुरंगों से आतंकियों को आने से रोकने के लिए गहन निगरानी के उपकरणों की आवश्यकता भी क्यों नहीं है ?
.
(टिपण्णी --- मेक्सिको की सीमा से अवैध रूप से श्रमिक बड़ी तादाद में अमेरिकी सीमा में प्रवेश करते है, क्योंकि अमेरिका के धनिक श्रमिक दर को कम रखना चाहते है। और अमेरिकी नागरिकों के पास संघीय सरकार को नियंत्रित करने के लिए आवश्यक क़ानून प्रक्रियाएं नहीं है। )
.
सवाल यह है कि -- सोनिया-मोदी-केजरीवाल भारत पाक की सीमा पर बेहद भारी भरकम पैसा खर्च करके दीवार बनाने का समर्थन कर रहे है, और साथ ही यह दावा भी कर रहे है कि इससे सीमा पार से आतंकियों की घुसपैठ में कमी आएगी। "मैं इस दीवार के निर्माण का विरोध नहीं करता हूँ"। कृपया इस बात को नोट कीजिये कि --- "मैं इस दीवार के निर्माण का विरोध नहीं करता हूँ"।
.
पर सवाल यह है कि -- क्या दीवार खड़ी किये जाने से आतंकवाद में कमी आएगी ? क्या पाकिस्तान के पास दीवार में छेद करने के लिए गोला-बारूद नहीं है ? और क्या आतंकियों को यह जानकारी नहीं है कि दीवार के नीचे से सुरंग निकाली जा सकती है ? इसके अलावा भौगोलिक परिस्थितियों जैसे जंगल, पहाड़ आदि पर विचार कीजिये। क्या ऐसे क्षेत्र में दीवार खड़ी करना आसान और कम खर्चीला होगा ?
.
और फेसबुक/व्हाट्स ऐप/अमेरिका के सहयोग से बड़े पैमाने पर कश्मीर के जो युवा आतंक का समर्थन करने के लिए प्रेरित हो रहे है ? ( कृपया इन आतंकियों की शवयात्राओं में उमड़ी भीड़ को देखें )
.
समाधान ?
.
हमें इस दीवार से कोई उल्लेखनीय लाभ नहीं होगा। मैं इस दीवार का विरोध नहीं करता। क्योंकि इससे हम पर सिर्फ मौद्रिक भार पड़ेगा। लेकिन भारत की अर्थव्यवस्था में पहले से ही इतने सारे और इतने बड़े छेद है और जिनसे इतना पैसा रिस रहा है कि इस दीवार पर किये जाने वाले कुछ हजार करोड़ के खर्चे पर हाय तौबा मचाने की कोई तुक नहीं।
.
समस्या का समाधान यह है कि हम अमेरिका का अनुसरण करे। अमेरिका को मेक्सिको सीमा पर ऐसी किसी दीवार की जरूरत इसीलिए नहीं है क्योंकि मैक्सिको में कोई आतंकी नेटवर्क नहीं है !!! और मेक्सिको में आतंकी नेटवर्क इसीलिए नहीं है क्योंकि ऐसे नेटवर्क को पनपाने के लिए राजनैतिक सरंक्षण की जरूरत होती है। और अगर मैक्सिको का कोई नेता मेक्सिको में ऐसे किसी आतंकी समूह को सरंक्षण देता जो कि अमेरिका में घुसपैठ करे, तो इस बात की गारंटी है कि अमेरिका की सेना अमुक नेता को मेक्सिको के शहर में ही सार्वजनिक रूप से फांसी पर लटका देती।
.
दूसरे शब्दों में पाकिस्तान की सीमा से आतंकियों की घुसपैठ रोकने का उपाय दीवार बनाना नहीं है, बल्कि भारत की सेना को उस हद तक मजबूत करना है कि भारत की सेना आतंक का समर्थन करने वाले नेताओ को लाहौर, इस्लामाबाद और कराची में जाकर सार्वजनिक फांसी दे सके। इतनी ताकतवर सेना का गठन हम कैसे कर सकते है ? "मेड इन इण्डिया, मेड बॉय इन्डियन" की नीति को लागू करते हुए स्वदेशी आधुनिक हथियारों का उत्पादन करके। कैसे हम ऐसे जटिल स्वदेशी आधुनिक हथियारों का उत्पादन कर पाएंगे ? भारत की निर्माण इकाइयों की क्षमता को बेहतर बनाकर। कैसे ? -- वेल्थ टैक्स, ज्यूरी सिस्टम, एमआरसीएम, जनमत संग्रह, राईट टू रिकॉल कानूनों को लागू करके।
.
और कैसे हम इन कानूनों को देश में लागू करवा सकते है ? उपाय सरल है -- कार्यकर्ताओ को कांग्रेस-बीजेपी-आप-संघ-सीपीएम आदि राजनैतिक समूहों को भाड़ में झोंक देना चाहिए। क्योंकि ये समूह 1920 से ही इन कानूनों का विरोध कर रहे है। असल में ये समूह उन सभी कानूनों के पैदाइशी विरोधी है जिससे भारत में तकनिकी विकास हो और भारत स्वदेशी हथियारों का उत्पादन करके आत्मनिर्भर हो सके। देश में सिर्फ "राईट टू रिकॉल पार्टी" ही एक मात्र पार्टी है जो इन कानूनों का समर्थन करती है। अत: कार्यकर्ताओ को राइट टू रिकॉल पार्टी के कार्यकर्ताओ के साथ काम करना चाहिए।
.
इसीलिए यदि सोनिया-मोदी-केजरीवाल दीवार बनाकर कुछ हजार करोड़ रूपये फूंकना चाहते है तो उन्हें फूंकने दो। मैं इस दीवार का विरोध नहीं करता। लेकिन मेरे विचार में यह पूरी तरह अनुपयोगी है। समाधान यह है कि हम भारत की सेना को मजबूत और आत्मनिर्भर बनाने के लिए आवश्यक कानूनों को गैजेट में प्रकाशित करे।
.

Pawan Jury BhilwaraRj

Tue Jul 12 2016 18:18:53 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

नमस्कार, Bittoo Suryavanshi ji,
.
यह स्तम्भ आपके प्रश्न तथा टिप्पणियों के लिए बनाया गया है।
.
सभी के लिए ,
.
यह पोस्ट सिर्फ bitto ji के प्रश्न और टिप्पणियों के लिए है। अन्य के प्रश्न और टिप्पणियाँ डिलीट कर दी जायेगी।
.
------------

प्रश्नकर्ता से निम्नलिखित नियमों के पालन की अपेक्षा की जाती है :
.
(1) हिन्दी भाषा के लिए हिन्दी लिपि और अंग्रेजी भाषा के लिए अंग्रेजी लिपि का प्रयोग करें। रोमन भाषा का प्रयोग नहीं करे ।
.
(2) दो पेराग्राफ के बीच '.' चिन्ह का प्रयोग करे ।
.
(3) लेखन में अनावश्यक चिन्हों जैसे '.......', '!!!!!!!', '//////', '####', '„„„„„„', '??????' आदि का तथा अनावश्यक 'केपिटल लेटर्स' का प्रयोग नहीं करें ।
.
(4) एक बार में एक ही कमेन्ट करे । यदि आपने दो कमेन्ट एक साथ किये है तो,
अ) पहले कमेन्ट को एडिट करें
ब) दुसरे कमेन्ट को कोपी करके पहले वाले कमेन्ट में पेस्ट करे
स) फिर दुसरे कमेन्ट को डिलीट कर दें ।
.
(5) कमेन्ट क्रमिक/सिलसिलेवार ढंग से करे, अक्रमिक रूप से नही ।
.
क्रमिक कमेन्ट क्या है ?
.
मान लीजिये कि
अ) आपने 'पहला' कमेन्ट किया है,
ब) मैंने 'दूसरा' कमेन्ट किया है,
स) आपने 'तीसरा' कमेन्ट किया है,
द) आपने चौथा कमेन्ट किया है ।
तो यहाँ पहला, दूसरा और तीसरा क्रमिक जबकि चौथा कमेन्ट अक्रमिक है । अक्रमिक कमेन्ट को बिन्दु 4 में बताये तरीके से संपादित कर पिछले कमेन्ट में जोड़ दें ।
.
(6) अपने प्रश्नों तथा टिप्पणियों को डिलीट नहीं करे । ताकि अन्य कार्यकर्ता भी इन्हें पढ़ सके ।
.
(7) कृपया मेरी फेसबुक प्रोफाइल पर नोट्स (Notes) सेक्शन में जाए तथा ' नागरिको को चाहिए कि वे अपने सांसद को SMS से आदेश भेजे' स्तम्भ को पढ़े । यह हमारे अभियान का केंद्र बिन्दु है । कृपया rahulmehta .com/301 .h. htm पर हमारे द्वारा प्रस्तावित क़ानून ड्राफ्ट्स का भी अध्ययन करें ।
.
(8) नोट्स सेक्शन में ही 'विशेष आलेख-01' शीर्षक से दर्ज स्तम्भ में राईट टू रिकाल ग्रुप द्वारा प्रस्तावित कुछ मुख्य कानूनी ड्राफ्ट्स की सूची तथा उनके लिंक देखे जा सकते है ।
.
(9) इस स्तम्भ का लिंक देखने के लिए नोट्स सेक्शन में जाकर 'आपसी संवाद स्तम्भ' या 'two person conversation thread' शीर्षक से रखे गए स्तम्भ को देखें । इस स्तम्भ का लिंक वहां दर्ज किया गया है ।
.
(10) हम देश में व्यवस्था परिवर्तन के लिए आवश्यक जूरी सिस्टम/राईट टू रिकाल कानूनों का मुक्त रूप से प्रचार करते है ताकि करोड़ो नागरिको की सहमति से इन कानूनों को गेजेट में प्रकाशित कराया जा सके । आप से अपेक्षा की जाती है कि प्रश्न/कमेंट्स पठनीय भाषा में रखें ।
.

What difference will your order to MLA via SMS make? Well, difference will come when 45 core voters send order to MLA via SMS. Now what difference will SMS from 45 crores voters of India to then MLAs will make? Where are ph# of MLA? All FAQs are answered in the first comment. To get link, pls see the pinned post of this page.
..
================
.
MESSAGE TO MLA
---------------------------
.
Dear MLA,
.
If you got the link to this status via SMS , then it is an ORDER sent to you via your voter. The author of this status post has NOTHING to do with this order. The order is to print following law-draft in the State Govt Gazette. Pls order CM to print this law-draft in the State Govt Gazette. Please see link and filehashes in first lines of this status
.
======= start of the law-draft ====
.
PLD.0035.RtrDCPj - Right to Recall District Court Principal judge law draft
.
[ Comments :
.
(C.1.1) These comments are not part of the law-draft. They are only to provide information to the activists, voters and Jurors.
.
(C.1.2) This law-draft refers to following proposed law-drafts -
.
(a) RtrPm
(b) RtrSCCj
(c) TcpIndia
(d) TcpState
.
This law-draft is similar to RtrSCCj. And knowing RtrPm and RtrSCCj drafts is necessary to understand this law-draft. But knowing other law-drafts isnt necessary to understand this law-draft. The fullnames and hashes of above law-drafts is given at the end in Appendix-I.
.
(C.1.3) CM can print this draft in State Govt Gazette. This doesnt require any law in Assembly or Parliament. The citizens may ask CM to print RtrDCPj law-draft using TcpState.
.
end of comments]
.
(1) [ general instructions ] The word citizen would mean a registered voter in the District. The word 'may' means 'may or need not', and clearly means 'no binding'. DCPj means District Court Principal judge. SCCj means Supreme Court Chief judge. SCj means a Supreme Court judge.
.
(2) [ proedure for DC = District Collector ] If any citizen of India above age of 30 years wishes to become DRJ = District Recognized Jurist, and he appears in person or via a lawyer with affidavit before DC, then DC would accept his candidacy for DRJ after taking filing fee same as deposit amount for MP election. Any existing judge in India may register himself as DRJ candidate for no fee.
.
[ comment - clauses-3 to clauses-5 are similar to RtrPM law-draft, wrt to DCCj. An activist or voter familiar with RtrPM may skip these clauses in first reading. ]
.
(3) [ procedure for Talati = Patwari = Village Officer or Talati's Clerks ]
.
(3.1) If a citizen comes in person to Talati's office, pays Rs 3 fee, and approves at most ten persons for the DRJ position, then the Talati would enter his approvals in the computer and would give him a receipt with his voter-id#, date/time and the persons he has approved. The fee shall be Rs 1 for those with BPL card.
.
(3.2) If a citizen comes to cancel his Approvals, the Talati will cancel one of more of his approvals without any fee.
.
(3.3) DC may create a system of sending SMS feedback to the voter.
.
(3.4) DC may create a system of taking finger-print and picture of the voter and putting it on the receipt.
.
(3.5) PM may add means to enable citizens to register approvals via SMS. PM may create a system where by citizens can register approvals via ATM.
.
(4) [ procedure for Talati ] The Talati will put the preferences of the citizen on district's website with citizen's voter-ID number and his preferences.
.
(5) [ procedure for DC ] On every Monday, DCs will publish Approval counts for each candidate.
.
(6) [ procedure for CM ] If a candidate gets approval of over 35% of registered citizen-voters in a District, then CM may appoint him as DRJ for that District.
.
(7) [ procedure for CM ] If an DRJ gets approval of over 55% of all voters in the District and the Approval count is 5% more than approval count of existing DCPj, then CM may send the name of the most approved DRJ to the Chief Judge of High Court asking him if he is appropriate for the position of DCPj. CM may also send the name to the PM. PM may send the name to SCCj.
.
(8) [ procedure for PM , all Loksabha MPs]
.
(8.1) If the HCCj and every other HCjs agree that the most approved DRJ should be new DCPJ and the existing DCPj resigns or is transferred, within 30 days, and SCCj and other SCjs agree with the appointment, then the PM may take no action.
.
(8.2) However, if HCCjs or any HCj or SCCj or any SCj opposes the appointment of that DRJ as DCPJ, or appointment doesnt happen within 30 says or gets stayed, then CM and PM may cancel their recommendations. And PM and all Loksabha MPs may resign and may declare new Loksabha election. Or, PM and MPs may declare an Emergency, as per our Magnum Opus Constitution of India, and temporariliy suspend all the Fundamental Rights, of all citizens of India, including all Supreme Court judges. The decision of PM and MPs will be final.
.
(9) [procedure for CM , PM] With approval of 51% of ALL voters of the State using TcpState, the CM may suspend these law-draft in any District for 5 years. And with approval of 45 crore voters in India via TcpIndia, PM may suspend this law-draft in any District/State for 5 years. And during these 5 years, HCCjs will appoint DCPjs.
.
[ comment - CV clauses are similar to TcpState. ]
.
(CV.1)[ procedure for DC ] If any citizen wants a change in this law-draft, he may submit an affidavit at DC's office and DC or his clerk will post affidavit on CM's website for Rs 20/- per page.
.
(CV.2) [ procedure for Talati or Patwari ] If any citizen want to register his opposition to this law or any section or wants to register YES-NO to affidavit submitted in above clause, Talati will enter YES/NO and give him a receipt for Rs 3 fee. The YES-NO will be posted on CM's website.
.

======= start of the law-draft ====
.
PLD.0036.RtrCityMayor - Right to Recall City Mayor law draft
.
[ Comments :
.
(C.1.1) These comments are not part of the law-draft. They are only to provide information to the activists, voters and Jurors.
.
(C.1.2) This law-draft refers to following proposed law-drafts -
.
(a) RtrCM
(b) TcpState
(c) TcpIndia
.
This law-draft is similar to RtrCM. And knowing RtrCM draft is necessary to understand this draft. But knowing other draft isnt must to understand this draft. The fullnames and hashes of above law-drafts is given at the end in Appendix-I.
.
(C.1.3) CM can print this draft in State Govt Gazette. This doesnt require any law in Assembly or Parliament. The citizens may ask CM to print this law-draft using TcpState.
.
end of comments]
.
(1) [ general instructions ] The word citizen would mean a registered voter in the Municipality or Municipal Corporation. The word 'may' means 'may or need not', and clearly means 'no binding'.
.
(2) [ proedure for DC = District Collector ] If any citizen of India above age of 25 years wishes to become Mayor, and he appears in person or via a lawyer with affidavit before DC, then DC would accept his candidacy for Mayor after taking filing fee same as deposit amount for MP election. An existing Mayor can also register for no fee.
.
[ comment - clauses-3 to clauses-5 are similar to RtrPM law-draft, wrt to Mayor. An activist or voter familiar with RtrPM may skip these clauses in first reading. ]
.
(3) [ procedure for Talati = Patwari = Village Officer or Talati's Clerks ]
.
(3.1) If a citizen comes in person to Talati's office, pays Rs 3 fee, and approves at most 5 persons for Mayor position, then the Talati would enter his approvals in the computer and would give him a receipt with his voter-id#, date/time and the persons he has approved. The fee shall be Rs 1 for those with BPL card.
.
(3.2) If a citizen comes to cancel his Approvals, the Talati will cancel one of more of his approvals without any fee.
.
(3.3) DC may create a system of sending SMS feedback to the voter.
.
(3.4) DC may create a system of taking finger-print and picture of the voter and putting it on the receipt.
.
(3.5) PM may add means to enable citizens to register approvals via SMS. PM may create a system where by citizens can register approvals via ATM.
.
(4) [ procedure for Talati ] The Talati will put the preferences of the citizen on district's website with citizen's voter-ID number and his preferences.
.
(5) [ procedure for DC ] On every Monday, DCs will publish Approval counts for each candidate.
.
(6) [ instruction to Mayor ] If a candidate has approval count is - (i) over 35% of all voters in Municipal Corporation and (ii) it is 5% (of all voters) more than approval count of existing Mayor (iii) it is 5% (of all voters) more than votes obtained by corporators who support the Mayor - then the Mayor may or may not resign. His decision will be final. And the corporators may or need not elect the candidate with hightest approval count as new Mayor. The decisions of Corporators will be final.
.
(6a) Example - Say city voter list has 40 lakh voters and say 192 Corporators. Say 120 Corporators support the Mayor and sum of the votes they had obtained in their election was say 10 lakhs. Say Mayor got approval directly from citizen which is say 9 lakh. Then approval count of Mayor will be higher of two i.e. 10 lakhs. Now if any alternate candidate gets 5% (of TOTAL i.e. 40 lakhs) i.e. 2 lakh more approvals, i.e. if he gets 12 lakh or more approvals, then the existing Mayor may resign.
.
(7) [procedure for CM , PM] With approval of 51% of ALL voters of the State using TcpState, CM may suspend these law-draft in any City for 5 years. And with approval of 45 crore voters in India via TcpIndia, PM may suspend this law-draft in any District/State for 5 years.
.
[ comment - CV clauses are similar to TcpState. ]
.
(CV.1)[ procedure for DC ] If any citizen wants a change in this law-draft, he may submit an affidavit at DC's office and DC or his clerk will post affidavit on CM's website for Rs 20/- per page.
.
(CV.2) [ procedure for Talati or Patwari ] If any citizen want to register his opposition to this law or any section or wants to register YES-NO to affidavit submitted in above clause, then the Talati will enter YES/NO and give him a receipt for Rs 3 fee. The YES-NO will be posted on CM's website.
.

PLD.0036.DCJurySys -- District Court Jury System
FileHash = b3035ee77f7eba8c32785a2d97819a73f2f0a705
BitTorrentHash = 020fc292568ba0e079db12635218a9a23180b611
FileLink = https://fb.com/groups/ProposedLawDrafts/1718029511747712

.
This status post describes the proposed law-draft 'District Court Jury System' . Pls see text of the law-draft below.

MESSAGE TO MP
---------------------------
.
Dear MP,
.
If you got the link to this status via SMS , then it is an ORDER sent to you via your voter. The author of this status post has NOTHING to do with this order. The order is to print following law-draft in the Central Govt Gazette. Pls order PM to print this law-draft in the Central Govt Gazette. To get the link to the file and filehashes, pls see first 5 lines of this status post
.
======= start of the law-draft ====
.
PLD.0151.DCJurySys - Jury System in District and Tahsil courts for civil, criminal and all other cases
.
[ Comments :
.
(C.1.1) These comments are not part of the law-draft. They are only to provide information to the activists, voters and Jurors.
.
(C.1.2) Summary - Once this law is printed by Parliament, the Lower Court cases will be decided by 15 to 1500 voters i.e. Jurors between the age of 25 years amd 55 years chosen at random from District / State / India. The judges will play only advisiory role. The lawyers and technical experts can also advise the Juriors. The final decision on facts and law will be taken by Jurors and Jurors. And one Jury of 15 to 1500 Jurors will decide only one case. And one voter who has served in a Jury cannot come again in Jury for next 10 years.
.
(C.1.3) This law-draft refers to following proposed law-drafts -
.
(a) TcpIndia
(b) TcpState
(c) TcpDistrict
.
The hashes of above law-drafts is given in Appendix-I.
.
(C.1.4) This law will need legislation in Parliament, but not a constitutional amendment. The activists and citizens should pass this law using TcpIndia.
.
end of comments]
.
section-1 : Right to Recall DJA = District Jury Administrator
.
[comment : The voters \ activists familiar with RTR procedure code may skip reading this section in first reading]
.
(1.1) [ general instruction ] The word citizen would mean a registered voter residing in the District. The word 'may' clearly means 'may or need not' and also clearly means 'not binding'. Approval filing will be open i.e. choices made by a voter will be visible to all with his voter-id and name on a designated website.
.
(1.2) [ procedure for CM ] The CM will appoint an officer as DJA for each District.
.
(1.3) [ procedure for DC = District Collector] If any citizen wishes to be DJA, he may appear in person or place an affidavit before DC. The DC is hereby ordered to accept his candidacy for DJA after taking fee same as deposit amount for MP election. DC will issue him a serial number.
.
(1.4) [ procedure for Talati = Patwari = Village Officer or his clerk ] A citizen can come in person to Talati, pay Rs 3 fee and approve at most five persons for DJA position. The Clerk will enter his approvals in the computer and issue him a receipt with his voter-id#, date/time and the persons he approved.
.
(1.5) [ procedure for Talati ] The Talati will put the preferences of the citizens on PM's website with citizen's voter-ID number and his preferences. i.e. Approval filing will be open.
.
(1.6) [ procedure for Talati ] If a citizen comes to cancel his Approvals, the Talati will cancel one or more of his approvals without any fee.
.
(1.7) [ procedure for DC ] On every Monday, DC will publish approval counts for each DJA candidate.
.
(1.8) [ procedure for CM ] If a DJA candidate gets approval of over 50% of ALL registered citizen-voters (ALL, not just those who have filed their approval) in District, and is 2% more than existing DJA, then CM may not expel the existing DJA and appoint the person with highest approval count as DJA.
.
(1.9) [ procedure for CM] If DJA's approval is below 33% of all voters, then CM may replace DJA with his appointee. But as long as approval is above 33%, CM need not replace him with his appointee. Discretion of CM will be final.
.
(1.10) [ procedure for CM ] With approval of over 51% of ALL citizen voters in that State, CM can suspend all above clauses and appoint his own DJA for 5 years in one or more districts. CM may change DJA anytime during these 5 years.
.
(1.11)[ procedure for PM ] With approval of over 51% of ALL citizen voters in India, PM can suspend all above cluases for any districts in India and appoint DJA for 5 years in those districts. PM may change DJA anytime during these 5 years.
.
section-2 : Formation of District Grand Jury
.
(2.1) [ procedure for DJA ] Formation of First Grand Jury - Using the voter list, DJA will, in a public meeting, randomly select 50 citizens from the voter-list between age of 25 years and 55 years. From that, he can exclude any 20 after interview, so that finally there are 30 Grand Jurors. If DJA is appointed by CM or PM under last 2 caluses of section-1, then DJA may select up to 100 citizens from District / State / India and exclude 70.
.
(2.2) [ procedure for DJA ] Formation of subsequent Grand Juries - In the first set of Grand Jurors, DJA will retire the first 10 Grand Jurors every 10 days. And after first month, term of each Grand Juror will be 3 months, and thus 10 Grand Jurors will retire every month and 10 more will be selected. For selection of 10 Grand Jurors, DJA will select 20 voters at random from voter list of District or State or India, as in clause above, and select any 10 after interview.
.
(2.3) [ procedure for DJA ] Method to select voters at random -
.
(2.3.a) The DJA will not use any electronic device to select a number randomly. He will use the procedure detailed by CM or PM. If CM or PM have not specified the procedure, he will select as follow.
.
(2.3.b) Say DJA has to choose a number between 1 and a four digit number say ABCD. Then DJA will do 4 rounds of dice-throw for each digit. In a round if the digit he needs to select is between 0-5, then DJA will use only 1 dice and if the digit he needs to select is between 0-9, he will use 2 dices.
.
(2.3.c) The number selected will be 1 less than the number which comes in case of single-dice throw and 2 less in case of double-dice throw. If throw of dices exceeds the highest digit he needs, he will throw the dices again.
.
(2.3.d) Example - Suppose DJA needs to select a page in a book, which has 3693 pages. Then DJA will execute 4 rounds. In the 1st round he will use 1 dice as he needs to select a number between 0-3. If the dice shows 5 or 6, he will throw the dice again. If the dice show 3, the number selected is 3-1=2. And DJA will proceed to second round. In the second round, he needs to select a number between 0-6. So he will throw two dices. If the sum exceeds 8, he will throw the dices again. If the sum is suppose 6, the second digit selected is 6 - 2 = 4. Like that, suppose the dices in 4 rounds show 3, 5, 10 and 2. Then DJA will select digits as (3 - 1), (5 - 2), (10 - 2), (2 - 1) i.e. page number 2381. The DJA should use different citizens to throw dices. Suppose the voter-list has B books, the largest book has P pages and all pages have N entries. Then using above method or method described by CM, DJA will select 3 random numbers between (1 to B), (1 to P) and (1 to N). Now suppose selected book has less than that many pages or the selected page has fewer entries. Then he will again select a numbers between (1 to B), (1 to P) and (1 to N).
.
(2.3.e) Another method can be - DJA can prepare list of all voters, and select two large prime numbers at random. Say there are N voters. Then DJA can select two prime numbers between N/2 and 2N say n and m. And the voters seelected can be (n mod N), (n + m) mod N, (n + 2m) mod N to (n + (k-1)*m) mod N, where k is number of persons to be selected.
.
(2.3.f) DJA or PM or CM will decide the procedure code to select Jurors.
.
(2.3.g) If DJA is appointed by CM or PM , then he may select voters from whole State or India. The age will be between 25 years and 55 years.
.
(2.4) [ procedure for DJA ] The Grand Jurors will meet on every Saturday and Sunday. They may meet on more days if over 15 Grand Jurors approve. The number must be over 15, even when less than 30 Grand Jurors are present. The meetings, if happen, must start at 11am and last till at least 5pm. The Grand Juror will get Rs. 500 per day he attends. The CM , PM may change the compensation as per inflation or other conditions such as travel distance. The money will be give 60 days after his term ends.
.
(2.5) [ procedure for DJA ] If a Grand Juror is absent on a meeting, he will not his daily payment. And may loose up to thrice his amount to be paid and maximum fine of 0.05% of his wealth and 1% of his annual income may be imposed. The individuals who are Grand Jurors 30 days later will decide the fine.
.
section-3 : Making accusation against a person or a company or a govt body or any entity
.
(3.1) [ procedure for DJA ] If any person or company or District Prosecutor or any entity, has complaint against any other person or entity, then he can write to some or all Grand Jurors or ask Grand Jurors to give hearing. The complainer must specify the remedy he wishes. The remedy can be obtaining possession of a property or obtaining monetary compensation from the accused or imprisoning the accused for certain number of years/months or his execution or any other remedy available in law.
.
(3.2) [ procedure for DJA ] If over 15 Grand Jurors, in a meeting, issue an invitation to a witness or complainer or accused, then that person may appear. The Grand Jurors may or need not invite the accused or complainer. They may allow lawyers and experts to speak or not to speak.
.
(3.3) [ procedure for DJA ] If over 15 Grand Jurors declare that there is some merit in the complaint, then the DJA will call a Jury consisting of 15 to 1500 citizens, between the age of 25 years and 55 years, to examine the complaint. And if less that 15 Grand Jurors see merit in the complain, then the complain will be dismissed
.
(3.4) [ procedure for Grand Jurors ] if majority Grand Jurors think that the complaint is absolutely frivilous then they may impose a fine of upto Rs 1000 per hour they spent in deliberation and no more. Each Grand Juror will decclare a fine, and the median fine will be imposed and excecuted by DJA. The Jurors will also decide how much of that fine should be given to wrongfully accused as compensation. The accused may file a seperate case to recover additional compensation for damages such as loss of time and other damages he suffered.
.
section-4 : Deciding number of Jurors in Jury and Jury slection
.
(4.1) [procedure for Grand Jurors and DJA] Deciding number of Jurors for a case - Each Grand Juror will propose the number of Jurors needed for the case, and DJA will take the median as the decision. In case number of Grand Jurors are even, then DJA will take the higher median. Each Juror will propose a number between 15 and 1500. The guideline for Grand Jurors are -
.
(4.1.a) if the accused has higher economic or social or political standing, then the number of Jurors may be higher.
.
(4.1.b) if the crime is serious, the number of Jurors should be higher. eg if crime is theft of say Rs 10000, then number of Jurors may be 15. And if the alleged crime is murder, then number of Jurors may be 50 or 100 or more.
.
(4.1.c) if the person is accused of multiple crimes in past, and Grand Jurors see merit in most complaints, then they may put higher number of Jurors. eg if a person appears as career criminal and has been accused for several beatings or murders in past, then number of Jurors against him can be 100 or even 1500.
.
(4.1.d) if amount involved is large, then number of Jurors may be more. Number should be 15 at least. And 1 more Juror for every Rs 1 crore may be added. The maximum can be 1500 Jurors.
.
(4.1.e) The decision of Grand Jurors on number of Jurors will be final.
.
(4.1.f) The Grand Jurors will decide whether the Jurors will be from District , State or India.
.
(4.2) [procedure for DJA ] Jury slection - The DJA will randomly select number of citizens equal to twice the number of Jurors required from the voter lists. Then DJA will then send them summors to appear. And of those who arrive, DJA will excuse the Jurors who are relatives or neighbors or colleagues of either party or their lawyers. And he will also excuse Jurors who have served in the District in past 10 years. Then DJA will select required number of Jurors at random, not using any interview. The decision on excusing will be taken by DJA and can be changed only by majority of Grand Jurors.
.
(4.3) [ procedure for DJA ] DJA will ask CDJ = Chief District Judge to appoint one or at most 3 Judges to advise the Jurors and administer the case. If the property in dispute is worth above Rs 1 crore or compensation claim is above Rs 10 lakhs or the maximum prison sentence applicable is above 24 months, then DJA will request Chief Judge to appoint 3 judges. The Chief Judge's decision will be final.
.
section-5 : Conducting trial hearings
.
(5.1) [ procedure for Presiding Judge ] The trial will go from 11am to 5pm. The trial will start only after majority of the Jurors selected (not just present) vote for hearing to start. In all clauses, the majority means majority of Jurors selcted, not just present.
.
(5.2) [ procedure for Presiding Judge ] The Judge will allow the complainer to speak for 1 hour, during which no can interrupt. Then Judge will allow the accused to speak for 1 hour and 30 minutes during which no one can interrupt. Like this, the Judge will alternate the speakers. The lunch break will start between 1 pm and 2pm and will be for 1 hour. The case will go on like this on every day. The Jurors with majority vote can change the duration for which one may speak.
.
(5.3) [ procedure for Presiding Judge ] The case will go for at least 2 days. On the 3rd day or later, if majority Jurors declare that they have heard enough, then the hearings will go on for 1 more day. If on the next day, majority Jurors declare that they would like to hear more arguments, then the case will go on till majority Jurors say that case should end.
.
(5.4) [ procedure for Presiding Judge ] On the last day, after both parties have presented the case for 1 hour each, the Jurors will deliberate for at least 2 hours. If after 2 hours, majority Jurors say that they need no more deliberation, then the Judge will ask each Juror to publicly declare his verdict.
.
(5.5) [ procedure for Presiding Judge ] In case of fine, each Juror will state the fine he thinks is appropriate, and must be less than the legal limit. If it is higher than legal limit, then the Judge must take it as legal limit. The Judge will arrange the fine amounts stated in decreasing order, i.e. highest first and lowest last. And the Judge will take the fine approved by over 2/3rd majority. eg if there are 100 Jurors, then 67th fine amount in decreasing order will be taken as the fine collectively imposed by the Jury. So if 34 out of 100 Jurors have declared zero fine, then fine will be zero.
.
(5.6) [ procedure for Presiding Judge ] In case of prison sentence, the Judge will arrange the imprisonment lengths cited by Jurors in decreasing order i.e. highest first and lowest last. If sentence length is higher than legal limit then maximum sentence as stated in the Law will be taken. And the Judge will take prinson senetnce approved by over 2/3rd of Jurors as prison sentence collectively decided by the Jury. So if 1/3rd Jurors have declared zero months of prison, then and then only accused will be declared not guilty and set free. eg if there are 100 Jurors, then 67th imprisonment length in decreasing order will be taken as the imprisonment. So if 34 Jurors say zero months, then punishment will be zero months.
.
(5.7) [ procedure for Presiding Judge ] In case of any other remedy asked, the Jurors will ask both parties to submit at most 5 proposals. Each Juror will give marks from 0 to 100 to each proposal. Not giving any marks would mean 0. The proposal with highest total marks would be taken as proposal approved by the Jurors.
.
(5.8) [ procedure for DJA ] DJA will give same compensation to Jurors as Grand Jurors
.
(5.9) [ procedure for Grand Jurors ] Fining a Juror for being absent or late - in case a Juror or a party does not show up or shows up late, the Grand Jurors after 3 months will decide the fine, which can be upto 1% of his wealth, or 10% of his annual income. The Grand Jurors decision will be final.
.
(5.10) [procedure for Jurors] If over 50% of Jurors think that complain was friviolous, then each Juror may impose a fine of Rs 300 per day per Juror and no more. Each Juror will disclose a fine, and fine approved by majority will be collected by DJA. Of this, accused will get amount equal to number of days he spent multiplied by his daily income in past income tax return. If the accused wants more compensation, then he may file a separate complain.
.
(5.11) [ instruction to all ] In complex cases, each party or DJA or judge may request any technical / legal expert to explains facts and law to the Jurors.
.
(5.12) [ procedure for DJA] DJA can create a Grand Jury at tahsil level and also run Jury Trial in tahsil courts, if that District has tahsil Courts. The Tahsil Court Grand Jury and Tahsil Court Jury will hear the cases that fall within them. DJA will appoint a TJA = Tahsil Jury administrator for Tahsil Courts. And operations will run in same way as decsribed in above clauses.
.
section-6 : The appeal and execution
.
(6.1) [ procedure for Accused, Complainer ] Either party will have 30 days to appeal against the verdict in the State's High Court or the Supreme Court of India. The verdict of Higher Court's Jurors will prevail over verdict of District Court Jury.
.
(6.2) [ procedure for DCPJ = District Court Principal Judge ] If citizens of district or state or India order District Principal Judge to cancel the verdict of District Court Jury using TcpDistrict or TcpState or TcpIndia, then DPCJ will cancel the verdict of Jury.
.
(6.3) [ procedure for DPC = District Police Chief ] The District Police Chief or policemen designated by him will execute the fine or imprisonments as written by the Judge and approved by the Jurors.
.
section-7 : Protection of Fundamental Rights of the Citizens
.
(7.1) [ procedure for All Govt Employees] No Govt employee will impose any fine or prison sentence without consent of over 2/3rd of Jurors of the Lower Courts, unless approved by the Jurors of High Courts or the Jurors or Supreme Court or majority voters of District / State / India. No Govt employee will imprison any citizen for more than 48 hours without approval of over 2/3rd of the Jurors or 50% of the Grand Jurors or majority voters of District / State / India using TcpDistrict / TcpState / TcpIndia
.
(7.2) [ instruction to everyone ] The Jurors will decide the facts as well as intensions, and must also interpret the laws as well the Constitution, and natural justice. If Jurors think that the law is unjust or inapplicable, then the Jurors can give not guilty verdict even against the law.
.
section-8 : Temporary suspension of District Jury System by voters of State or India
.
(8.1) [ procedure for CM] With approval of 51% voters in the State, CM can suspend District Court Jury System in a District for 5 years, and run all trials in that District in any other Districts he decides.
.
(8.2) [ procedure for PM] With approval of 51% voters in India, the PM can suspend District Court Jury System in a State or any District for 5 years, and run all trials in that State/District in any other Districts in any State/District he decides.
.
section-CV : Citizen's Voice
.
[ comment - this CV section is as similar to TcpIndia. An activist familaira with TcpIndia may skip this section].
.
(CV.1) [ procedure for DC ] If a citizen wants to propose any change in this law, then the citizen can submit an affidavit demanding the change to DC or his clerk who must post it on the website of Prime Minister for a fee of Rs 20 per page.
.
(CV.2) [ procedure for Talati = Patwari ] If a citizen wants to register his opposition to this law or any clause of this law or wants to register any support to affidavit filed in the above clause, then he may register his YES/No for a Rs 3 fee at Patwari's office. The Patwari will note the citizen's YES/NO and will also post the citizen's YES/NO on PM's website.
.

.

PLD.0152.HCJurySys --- High Court Jury System

MESSAGE TO MP
---------------------------
.
Dear MP,
.
If you got the link to this status via SMS , then it is an ORDER sent to you via your voter. The author of this status post has NOTHING to do with this order. The order is to print following law-draft in the Central Govt Gazette. Pls order PM to print this law-draft in the Central Govt Gazette. To get the link to the file and filehashes, pls see first 5 lines of this status post
.
======= start of the law-draft ====
.
PLD.0153.SCJurySys - Law draft to bring Jury System in all cases in Supreme Court of India
.
[ comments :
.
(C.1.1) These comments are not part of the law-draft. They are only to provide information to the activists, voters and Jurors.
.
(C.1.2) Summary - once this law is passed in Parliament, the judge system in Supreme Court for ALL cases in India will end. All judgments in Supreme Court will be given by 3 to 5 High Courts chosen at random OR by 15 to 1500 voters chosen at random from all over India. The judges will play only advisory role. The final decision will be taken by the Jurors. And one Jury will decide only one case. And one voter who has served in a Jury cannot come again in Jury for next 10 years.
.
(C.1.3) This law-draft refers to following proposed law-drafts :
.
(a) DCJurySys
(b) TcpIndia
.
Knowing all these drafts is necessary to understand this law-draft. The hashes of above law-drafts is given at the end in Appendix-I.
.
(C.1.4) This law-draft needs legislation in Parliament. No constitutional amendment is needed. And activists should try to get this law using TcpIndia.
.
end of comments]
.
section-1 : Registering citizens' approvals for NJA = National Jury Administrator candidates
.
[ comment - section-1 is similar to section-1 of DCJurySys, at national level. An activist may skip this in first reading.]
.
(1.1) [ general instruction ] The word citizen would mean a registered voter residing in the India. The word 'may' clearly means 'may or need not' and also clearly means 'not binding'. Approval filing will be open i.e. choices made by a voter will be visible to all with his voter-id and name on a designated website.
.
(1.2) [ procedure for PM ] PM would appoint an officer called as NJA = National Jury Administrator.
.
(1.3) [ procedure for DC = District Collector] If any citizen wishes to be NJA, he may appear in person or place an affidavit before DC. The DC is hereby ordered to accept his candidacy for NJA after taking fee same as deposit amount for MP election. DC will issue him a serial number.
.
(1.4) [ procedure for Talati = Patwari = Village Officer or his clerk ] A citizen can come in person to Talati, pay Rs 3 fee and approve at most five persons for JA position. The Clerk will enter his approvals in the computer and issue him a receipt with his voter-id#, date/time and the persons he approved.
.
(1.5) [ procedure for Talati ] The Talati will put the preferences of the citizens on PM's website with citizen's voter-ID number and his preferences. i.e. Approval filing will be open.
.
(1.6) [ procedure for Talati ] If a citizen comes to cancel his Approvals, then Talati will cancel one or more of his approvals without any fee.
.
(1.7) [ procedure for DC ] On every Monday, DC may publish approval counts for each NJA candidate.
.
(1.8) [ procedure for PM ] If a NJA candidate gets approval of over 45 crore voters in India and is 1 crore more than approval count of existing NJA, then PM may expel the existing NJA and appoint the person with highest approval count as new NJA.
.
(1.9) [ procedure for PM] If NJA's approval is below 25 crore voters, then PM may or need not replace NJA with his appointee. But as long as approval is above 33%, PM need not replace him with his appointee. Discretion of PM will be final.
.
section-2 : Formation of National Grand Jury
.
(2.1) [ procedure for NJA ] Formation of First Grand Jury - Using the India's voter list, NJA will, in a public meeting, randomly select 80 citizens between age of 35 years and 55 years from the voter-list. From that, of the first 60 who come to National Capital, he can exclude any 30 after interview, so that finally there are 30 National Grand Jurors.
.
(2.2) [ procedure for NJA ] Formation of subsequent Grand Juries - In the first set of Grand Jurors, NJA will retire the first 10 Grand Jurors every 10 days. And after first month, term of each Grand Juror will be 3 months, and thus 10 Grand Jurors will retire every month and 10 more will be selected. For selection of new 10 Grand Jurors, NJA will select 50 citizens at random from voter list and from first 20 who arrive, select any 10 after interview.
.
(2.3) [ procedure for NJA ] Random selection method - NJA will use method given in DCJurySys law-draft or method approved by PM or citizens using TcpIndia.
.
(2.4) [ procedure for NJA ] The National Grand Jurors will meet every day except Supreme Court Holidays. The meetings will be from 10 am to 5pm. The National Grand Juror will get Rs. 2000 per day he attends. The PM may change the compensation as per inflation or other conditions. The money will be given 60 days after his term ends. In addition, National Grand Jurors will get stay in Govt owned guest house with spouse and children, and will get flight fare to his home every weekend.
.
(2.5) [ procedure for NJA ] If a Grand Juror is absent in a meeting, he will not get his daily payment. And may loose up to thrice the daily amount to be paid and maximum fine of 0.01% of his wealth and 1% of his annual income may be imposed. The individuals who are Grand Jurors 30 days later will decide the fine.
.
section-3 : Accusing a person or a company or any entity
.
(3.1) [ procedure for NJA ] If any person or company or National Prosecutor or any entity, has complaint against any other person or entity, then he can write to all or some Grand Jurors. The complainer must specify the remedy he wishes. The remedy can be obtaining possession of a property or obtaining monetary compensation from the accused or imprisoning the accused for certain number of years/months or his execution or any other remedy available in law.
.
(3.2) [ procedure for NJA ] If over 15 Grand Jurors, in a meeting, issue an invitation to a witness or complainer or accused, then that person may appear. The Grand Jurors may or need not invite the accused or complainer. They may allow lawyer to speak or not to speak.
.
(3.3) [ procedure for NJA ] If less that 15 Grand Jurors see merit in the complain, then the complain will be dismissed. If over 15 Grand Jurors declare that there is some merit in the complaint, then NJA will ask each Grand Juror whether to call (a) a Jury consisting of 15 to 1500 randomly chosen citizens between age of 35 years and 55 years across all States of India OR (b) select 3 to 5 States at random and send case to their High Court Juries. And based on majority decision, NJA will execute (a) or (b)
.
(3.4) [ procedure for National Grand Jurors ] if Grand Jurors think that the complaint is absolutely frivilous then they may impose a fine of upto Rs 10000 per hour they spent in deliberation and no more. Each Grand Juror will decclare a fine, and the fine approved by majority will be imposed and excecuted by NJA. The Jurors will also decide how much of that fine should be given to wrongfully accused as compensation. The accused may file a seperate case to recover additional compensation for damages such as loss of time and other damages he suffered.
.
section-4 : Starting trial against accused
.
(4.1) [ procedure for Grand Jurors] if over 50% Grand Jurors think that trial by three or five State High Courts will suffice and trial by National Jury isnt needed, then the State Grand Jurors can ask NJA to send the case to three or five State Courts chosen at random. And in that case, the majority verdict will be taken as verdict of Supreme Court of India. The States chosen should not be neigboring, so that States are scattred across India.
.
(4.2) If National Grand Jurors opt for trial by National Grand Jury, then NJA and Grand Jurors will use the following clauses in this section.
.
(4.3) National Grand Jurors may decide number of Jurors that District Courts must select. Each Juror will specify a number and NJA will take the higher median.
.
(4.4) [procedure for NJA ] The NJA will select twice the number of Jurors required and send them summors to appear. The voters must be between the age of 35 years and 45 years. And of those who arrive, the NJA will excuse the Jurors who are relatives of contesting parties or relatives of lawyers of contesting parties or neighbbors residing within 500 meters or in same apartment complex of contesting parties. Then NJA will select required number of Jurors at random. The decision on excusing will be taken by NJA and can be changed by only by majority of Grand Jurors.
.
(4.5) [procedure for Grand Jurors and NJA] Deciding number of Jurors for a case - Each Grand Juror will propose the number of Jurors needed for the case , and NJA will take the median. In case number of Grand Jurors are even, then NJA will take the higher median. Each Juror will propose a number between 15 and 1500. The guideline for National Grand Jurors will be similar as in DCJurySys
.
(4.6)[ procedure for NJA ] NJA will ask SCCj = Supreme Court Chief judge to appoint one or at most 3 judges to preside over the case. If the property in dispute is worth above Rs 50 crores or compensation claim is above Rs. 5 crore or the maximum prison sentence is above 5 years, then NJA will request SCCj to appoint 3 judges. SCCj's decision will be final.
.
section-5 : Conducting trial hearings
.
(5.1) [ procedure for Presiding Judge ] The trial will go from 11am to 5pm. The trial will start only after majority of the Jurors selected (not just present) vote for hearing to start. In all clauses, the 'majority of Jurors' means majority of Jurors selcted, not just present.
.
(5.2) [ procedure for Presiding Judge ] The Judge will allow the complainer to speak for 1 hour, during which no can interrupt. Then Judge will allow the accused to speak for 1 hour and 30 minutes during which no one can interrupt. Like this, the Judge will alternate the speakers. The lunch break will start between 1 pm and 2pm and will be for 1 hour. The case will go on like this on every day. The Jurors with majority vote can change the duration for which one may speak.
.
(5.3) [ procedure for Presiding Judge ] The case will go for at least 2 days. On the 3rd day or later, if majority Jurors declare that they have heard enough, then the hearings will go on for 1 more day. If on the next day, majority Jurors declare that they would like to hear more arguments, then the case will go on till majority Jurors say that case should end.
.
(5.4) [ procedure for Presiding Judge ] On the last day, after both parties have presented the case for 1 hour each, the Jurors will deliberate for at least 2 hours. If after 2 hours, majority Jurors say that they need no more deliberation, then the Judge will ask each Juror to publicly declare his verdict.
.
(5.5) [ procedure for Presiding Judge ] In case of fine, each Juror will state the fine he thinks is appropriate, and must be less than the legal limit. If it is higher than legal limit, then the Judge must take it as legal limit. The Judge will arrange the fine amounts specified by Jurors in decreasing order, i.e. highest first and lowest last. And the Judge will take the fine approved by over 2/3rd majority. eg if there are 100 Jurors, then 67th fine amount in decreasing order will be taken as the fine collectively imposed by the Jury. So if 34 out of 100 Jurors have declared zero fine, then fine will be zero.
.
(5.6) [ procedure for Presiding Judge ] In case of prison sentence, the Judge will arrange the sentence lengths cited by Jurors. If sentence length is higher than legal limit then maximum sentence as specified in the Law will be taken. And the Judge will take prinson senetnce approved by 2/3rd of Jurors as prison sentence collectively decided by the Jury. So if 1/3rd Jurors have declared zero months of prison, then and then only accused will be declared not guilty and set free.
.
(5.7) [ procedure for Presiding Judge ] In case of any other remedy asked, the Jurors will ask both parties to submit at most 3 proposals. Each Juror will give marks from 0 to 100 to each proposal. Not giving any marks would be mean 0. The proposal with highest total marks would be taken as proposal approved by the Jurors.
.
(5.8) [ procedure for NJA ] NJA will give same compensation to Jurors as Grand Jurors
.
(5.9) [ procedure for Grand Jurors ] Fining a Juror for being absent or late - in case a Juror or a party does not show up or shows up late, the Grand Jurors after 3 months will decide the fine, which can be upto 1% of his wealth, or 10% of his annual income. The Grand Jurors decision will be final.
.
(5.10) [procedure for Jurors] If over 50% of Jurors think that complain was friviolous, then each Juror may impose a fine of Rs 5000 per day per Juror and no more. Each Juror will disclose a fine, and fine approved by majority will be collected by NJA. Of this fine collected, accused will get amount equal to number of days he spent multiplied by his daily income in past income tax return. If the accused wants more compensation, then he may file a separate complain.
.
(5.11) [ instruction to all ] In complex cases, each party or NJA or judge may request any technical / legal expert to explains facts and law to the Jurors.
.
section-7 : Appeal
.
(7.1) [ instruction to all ] The judgement of Supreme Court Jurors can be cancelled only by voters of India using TcpIndia or by Loksabha using Legislation. Otherwise, it will be final.
.
section-8 : Protection of a Fundamental Rights of the Citizens
.
(8.1) [ procedure for All Govt Employees] No Govt employee will impose any fine or prison sentence without consent of over 2/3rd of Jurors of the Lower Courts or the Jurors of High Courts or the Jurors or Supreme Court in that order. No Govt employee will imprison any citizen for more than 48 hours without approval of over 2/3rd of the Jurors or 50% of the Grand Jurors.
.
(8.2) [ instruction to everyone ] The Jurors will decide the facts as well as intensions, and shall also interpret the laws as well the Constitution, and natural justice.
.
section-CV : Citizen's Voice
.
[comment - similar to section-7 of DCJurySys]
.
(CV.1) [ procedure for DC ] If a citizen wants to propose any change in this law, then the citizen can submit an affidavit demanding the change to District Collector or his clerk who will post it on the website of Prime Minister for a fee of Rs 20 per page.
.
(CV.2) [ procedure for Talati = Patwari ] If a citizen wants to register his opposition to this law or any clause of this law or wants to register any support to affidavit filed in the above clause, then he may register his YES/No for a Rs 3 fee at Patwari's office. The Patwari will note the citizen's YES/NO and will also post the citizen's YES/NO on PM's website.
.
Appendix-I
.
(a) DCJurySys
Fullname = PLD.0151.DCJurySys
FileHash = b3035ee77f7eba8c32785a2d97819a73f2f0a705
BitTorrentHash = 020fc292568ba0e079db12635218a9a23180b611
.
(b) TcpIndia
Fullname = PLD.007.TcpIndia
FileHash = 03d83b97a1180fe88cc15bd6fd9771c1b3a551d8
BitTorrentHash = e65f846014a5c823dc521392bbf57b97fcf20dcb

And based on majority decision, NJA will execute (a) or (b)
.
(3.4) [ procedure for National Grand Jurors ] if Grand Jurors think that the complaint is absolutely frivilous then they may impose a fine of upto Rs 10000 per hour they spent in deliberation and no more. Each Grand Juror will decclare a fine, and the fine approved by majority will be imposed and excecuted by NJA. The Jurors will also decide how much of that fine should be given to wrongfully accused as compensation. The accused may file a seperate case to recover additional compensation for damages such as loss of time and other damages he suffered.
.
section-4 : Starting trial against accused
.
(4.1) [ procedure for Grand Jurors] if over 50% Grand Jurors think that trial by three or five State High Courts will suffice and trial by National Jury isnt needed, then the State Grand Jurors can ask NJA to send the case to three or five State Courts chosen at random. And in that case, the majority verdict will be taken as verdict of Supreme Court of India. The States chosen should not be neigboring, so that States are scattred across India.
.
(4.2) If National Grand Jurors opt for trial by National Grand Jury, then NJA and Grand Jurors will use the following clauses in this section.
.
(4.3) National Grand Jurors may decide number of Jurors that District Courts must select. Each Juror will specify a number and NJA will take the higher median.
.
(4.4) [procedure for NJA ] The NJA will select twice the number of Jurors required and send them summors to appear. The voters must be between the age of 35 years and 45 years. And of those who arrive, the NJA will excuse the Jurors who are relatives of contesting parties or relatives of lawyers of contesting parties or neighbbors residing within 500 meters or in same apartment complex of contesting parties. Then NJA will select required number of Jurors at random. The decision on excusing will be taken by NJA and can be changed by only by majority of Grand Jurors.
.
(4.5) [procedure for Grand Jurors and NJA] Deciding number of Jurors for a case - Each Grand Juror will propose the number of Jurors needed for the case , and NJA will take the median. In case number of Grand Jurors are even, then NJA will take the higher median. Each Juror will propose a number between 15 and 1500. The guideline for National Grand Jurors will be similar as in DCJurySys
.
(4.6)[ procedure for NJA ] NJA will ask SCCj = Supreme Court Chief judge to appoint one or at most 3 judges to preside over the case. If the property in dispute is worth above Rs 50 crores or compensation claim is above Rs. 5 crore or the maximum prison sentence is above 5 years, then NJA will request SCCj to appoint 3 judges. SCCj's decision will be final.
.
section-5 : Conducting trial hearings
.
(5.1) [ procedure for Presiding Judge ] The trial will go from 11am to 5pm. The trial will start only after majority of the Jurors selected (not just present) vote for hearing to start. In all clauses, the 'majority of Jurors' means majority of Jurors selcted, not just present.
.
(5.2) [ procedure for Presiding Judge ] The Judge will allow the complainer to speak for 1 hour, during which no can interrupt. Then Judge will allow the accused to speak for 1 hour and 30 minutes during which no one can interrupt. Like this, the Judge will alternate the speakers. The lunch break will start between 1 pm and 2pm and will be for 1 hour. The case will go on like this on every day. The Jurors with majority vote can change the duration for which one may speak.
.
(5.3) [ procedure for Presiding Judge ] The case will go for at least 2 days. On the 3rd day or later, if majority Jurors declare that they have heard enough, then the hearings will go on for 1 more day. If on the next day, majority Jurors declare that they would like to hear more arguments, then the case will go on till majority Jurors say that case should end.
.
(5.4) [ procedure for Presiding Judge ] On the last day, after both parties have presented the case for 1 hour each, the Jurors will deliberate for at least 2 hours. If after 2 hours, majority Jurors say that they need no more deliberation, then the Judge will ask each Juror to publicly declare his verdict.
.
(5.5) [ procedure for Presiding Judge ] In case of fine, each Juror will state the fine he thinks is appropriate, and must be less than the legal limit. If it is higher than legal limit, then the Judge must take it as legal limit. The Judge will arrange the fine amounts specified by Jurors in decreasing order, i.e. highest first and lowest last. And the Judge will take the fine approved by over 2/3rd majority. eg if there are 100 Jurors, then 67th fine amount in decreasing order will be taken as the fine collectively imposed by the Jury. So if 34 out of 100 Jurors have declared zero fine, then fine will be zero.
.
(5.6) [ procedure for Presiding Judge ] In case of prison sentence, the Judge will arrange the sentence lengths cited by Jurors. If sentence length is higher than legal limit then maximum sentence as specified in the Law will be taken. And the Judge will take prinson senetnce approved by 2/3rd of Jurors as prison sentence collectively decided by the Jury. So if 1/3rd Jurors have declared zero months of prison, then and then only accused will be declared not guilty and set free.
.
(5.7) [ procedure for Presiding Judge ] In case of any other remedy asked, the Jurors will ask both parties to submit at most 3 proposals. Each Juror will give marks from 0 to 100 to each proposal. Not giving any marks would be mean 0. The proposal with highest total marks would be taken as proposal approved by the Jurors.
.
(5.8) [ procedure for NJA ] NJA will give same compensation to Jurors as Grand Jurors
.
(5.9) [ procedure for Grand Jurors ] Fining a Juror for being absent or late - in case a Juror or a party does not show up or shows up late, the Grand Jurors after 3 months will decide the fine, which can be upto 1% of his wealth, or 10% of his annual income. The Grand Jurors decision will be final.
.
(5.10) [procedure for Jurors] If over 50% of Jurors think that complain was friviolous, then each Juror may impose a fine of Rs 5000 per day per Juror and no more. Each Juror will disclose a fine, and fine approved by majority will be collected by NJA. Of this fine collected, accused will get amount equal to number of days he spent multiplied by his daily income in past income tax return. If the accused wants more compensation, then he may file a separate complain.
.
(5.11) [ instruction to all ] In complex cases, each party or NJA or judge may request any technical / legal expert to explains facts and law to the Jurors.
.
section-7 : Appeal
.
(7.1) [ instruction to all ] The judgement of Supreme Court Jurors can be cancelled only by voters of India using TcpIndia or by Loksabha using Legislation. Otherwise, it will be final.
.
section-8 : Protection of a Fundamental Rights of the Citizens
.
(8.1) [ procedure for All Govt Employees] No Govt employee will impose any fine or prison sentence without consent of over 2/3rd of Jurors of the Lower Courts or the Jurors of High Courts or the Jurors or Supreme Court in that order. No Govt employee will imprison any citizen for more than 48 hours without approval of over 2/3rd of the Jurors or 50% of the Grand Jurors.
.
(8.2) [ instruction to everyone ] The Jurors will decide the facts as well as intensions, and shall also interpret the laws as well the Constitution, and natural justice.
.
section-CV : Citizen's Voice
.
[comment - similar to section-7 of DCJurySys]
.
(CV.1) [ procedure for DC ] If a citizen wants to propose any change in this law, then the citizen can submit an affidavit demanding the change to District Collector or his clerk who will post it on the website of Prime Minister for a fee of Rs 20 per page.
.
(CV.2) [ procedure for Talati = Patwari ] If a citizen wants to register his opposition to this law or any clause of this law or wants to register any support to affidavit filed in the above clause, then he may register his YES/No for a Rs 3 fee at Patwari's office. The Patwari will note the citizen's YES/NO and will also post the citizen's YES/NO on PM's website.
.
Appendix-I
.

BitTorrentHash = 020fc292568ba0e079db12635218a9a23180b611

PLD.0051.RtrMinisterIndia - Right to Recall Minister in Central Govt
.
[ Comments :
.
(C.1.1) These comments are not part of the law-draft. They are only to provide information to the activists, voters and Jurors.
.
(C.1.2) Summary - Once this law is printed in Gazette by PM, voters will get a procedure to expel / replace any Central Govt Minister except PM, Home Minister and Defence Minister. To replace PM, the proposed GN is RtrPM. And Home Ministry and Defence Ministry are so closely linked with PM, that separating them will disrutpt PM's functioning. And If citizens want to replace Home Minister or Defence Minister, then citizens can do so using TcpIndia anyway.
.

.

(b) TcpIndia
.

.
end of comments]
.
(1) [ general instruction ] The word citizen would mean a registered voter residing in India. The word 'may' clearly means 'may or need not' and also clearly means 'not binding'. Approval filing will be open i.e. choices given by a voter, along his voter-id and name, will be visible to all, on a designated website.
.
(2) [ instruction to DC = District Collector] if citizen of India wishes to become Minister in-charge of a Dept in Central Govt such as Chemicals, Petro Chemicals, Education, Transportation, Railways or any dept mentioned in the list at the end of this GN, and he appears in person or via a lawyer with affidavit before DC, then the Secretary or Office would accept his candidacy for Minister of that dept after taking filing fee twice as deposit amount for MP election.
.

.

.
(5) [ instruction to Talati ] If a the citizen comes to cancel his Approvals, the Talati will cancel one of more of his approvals without any fee.
.
(6) [ instructions to DC, PM] DC may create a system which will send SMS feedback to the voter. And PM may create a system where approvals can be registered over ATM or over SMS.
.
(7) [ instructions to DC] On every 5th of month, DC may publish Approval counts for each candidate as on last date of the previous month.
.
(8) [ instruction to PM ] If a candidate gets approval of over 43 crore voters, and it is 1 crore more than approval count of existing Minister, then PM may or need not expel the existing Minister and may or need not appoint the person with highest approval count as new Minister of that dept. The decision of PM will be final.

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Mon Jul 18 2016 13:48:00 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

Why sponsors of NaMo wanted GST to come in 2017 and not before?
.
GST starts killing small businessmen and manufacturers, but 2 to 4 years AFTER it is introduced , not till then !!! And for first 2 to 4 years, GST looks good because all taxes get combined into one tax, and raids and harassment of officers decrease. But after 2 to 4 years, due to missing trader problem, the small businesses (and manufacturers) will start dying.
.
So if GST had come as on 1-apr-2015, then for 2-3 years, all would have felt good. But by 2018, they would have all started suffering and become anti-NaMo.
.
But now, GST will come as on 1-apr-2017. So for next 2 years, all will feel good due to simplification and reduction in corruption / harassment. And so more and more small businessmen etc will supprt NaMo. And come jun-2019 and later, they will get slaughtered en-masse !!
.
What is missing trader problem?
.
In VAT = GST, say trader or manufacturer A sells goods to B for say price of Rs 1000 and later B sells to C for say Rs 1050. Now say VAT = GST rate is say 20%. Then A will collect Rs 200 GST from B and B will collect Rs 210 GST from C. Then A will have to deposit Rs 200 to govt and B will have to deposit Rs 10 to Govt.
.
BUT, if A vanishes, then for all practical purposes, B will have to pay Rs 200 to govt again !!!
.
The technical statement is "accessee will not get input credit if his vendor has not paid VAT = GST" which in simple language means "if your seller doesnt deposit GST = VAT you had paid to him, then please pay again, unless you the end buyer"
.
Now, SoGa and ArKe too both have supported GST with Missing Trader Clauses !! So on this issue , SoGa = NaMo = ArGandhi
.
So say GST comes as on 1-apr-2017. Say B bought goods from A in apr-2017 and A didint deposit GST. Then B will get notice in around may-2019, because there is often a 2 year procedural delay !! Because A has collected VAT from B in say apr-2016, and legally, he has 1 year time to pay that amount to govt. As such he has to pay within 3 months, but if he delays, then there is only some interest to be paid, and it isnt a crime.So GST = VAT dept will send A a default notice only in around may-2018, and the B will get "input credit denied" notice one year later, i.e. in may-2019. But by that time, he would have voted for NaMo !!!
.
Now during these two years, B will not even know that lightening is going to hit him in may-2019 !!! And between apr-2017 and march-2019, he will feel great because all taxes have merged into one tax, there is less paperwork, there is less harassment of officers, less bribes to be paid to officers etc. And come may-2019, the missing trader problem will simply slaughter him !!!
.
Now say GST had come in apr-2015. Then small traders and manufacturers would have started suffering right in may-2017, and support of NaMo would have started declining. And so sponsors of NaMo delayed GST for so long. (please note -- sponsors of NaMo and not NaMo. NaMo has no power to delay or speed up. NaMo, like SoGa and ArKe, is now just a puppet. SoMoke are kept so that Indian voters dont realize that foreign masters are now running the show.).
.
The missing trader problem is more likely to hit small manufactures/ traders then big ones,. Why? Necause the length of chain in small manufacturers/ business is LONGER than big businesses. eg consider WalMart. The length of its chain is just one --- it will import form china and sell in India. So missing trader problem will NOT at all effect WalMart. But consider some Indian manufacturer, who is buying raw material from detailer who buys from wholesler, And then that manufacturer sells it to some wholesaler who sells it to stoickist who sells to retailer. The length of chain from raw material to end user is at least 6 to 20 So now anyone of them commits a missing trader fraud or collapses and ends up creating missing trader problem, then whole chain will get disturbed.
.
SOLUTION?
.
Solution I propose is to kill VAT, GST, Excise, Service Tax and instead have (wealth tax - income tax) and have income tax minus plethora of hifi exemptions. So (wealth tax minus income tax minus 15% of salaries paid) will fall ONLY on those who have unused land. And once plethora of hifi exemptions like SEZ are removed, income tax rate will decrease. Further, wealth tax will cause fall in land prices, to 10% of what is now, and so businesses and manufacturing will grow.
.
At this point, I want ALL activists to know that SoMoKe have all opposed wealth tax and support GST with "buyer will pay for missing trader clauses". And every SoMoKe-andhbhagats and every CoBhApSwaRa-worker is also opposing WT and supporting GST with "buyer will pay for missing trader clauses". As of today in India, ONLY we activists at Right to Recall Party are support WT and opposing GST with or without "buyer will pay for missing trader clauses" . So if you support WT and oppose GST, then please dump SoMoke, SoMoKe-andhbhagats and CoBhApSwaRa-workers and pls join Right to recall party

PLD.0052.RtrMinisterState - Right to Recall Minister in State Govt
.
[ Comments :
.

.

.

(b) TcpState
.

.

.
(1) [ general instruction ] The word citizen would mean a registered voter residing in the State. The word 'may' clearly means 'may or need not' and also clearly means 'not binding'. Approval filing will be open i.e. choices given by a voter, along his voter-id and name, will be visible to all, on a designated website.
.
(2) [ instruction to DC = District Collector] if citizen of India, (whole of India, not just State) wishes to become Minister in-charge of a Dept in State Govt such as Chemicals, Education, Transportation or any dept mentioned in the list in this GN, and he appears in person or via a lawyer with affidavit before DC, then the DC would accept his candidacy for Minister of that dept after taking filing fee twice as deposit amount for MP election.
.
(3) [ instruction to Talati, or Talati's Clerks ] If a citizen of India comes in person to Talati's office, pays Rs 3 fee, and approves at most five persons for the position of a specific Ministry, then the Talati would enter his approvals in the computer and would give him a receipt with his voter-id#, date/time and the persons he approved.
.
(4) [ instruction to Talati ] The Talati will put the preferences of the citizen on CM's website with citizen's voter-ID number and his preferences. So approval filing is open.
.
(5) [ instruction to Talati ] If a the citizen comes to cancel his Approvals, the Talati will cancel one of more of his approvals without any fee.
.
(6) [ instructions to DC, CM] DC may create a system which will send SMS feedback to the voter. And CM may create a system where approvals can be registered over ATM or over SMS.
.
(7) [ instructions to DC] On every 5th of month, DC may publish Approval counts for each candidate as on last date of the previous month.
.
(8) [ instruction to CM ] If a candidate gets approval of over 51% of all voters in the State (all voters, not just the voters who have filed approvals), and it 5% more than approval count of existing Minister, then CM may or need not expel the existing Minister and may or need not appoint the person with highest approval count as new Minister of that dept. The decision of CM will be final.
.
(9) [instruction to all] List of Ministries and Departments covered under this GN
.
Home Ministry Departments are not covered in this GN. Only following dept come under GN, where citizens can directly replace Minister. The CM can change the list via any GN. And citizens may change the list using TcpState.
.
001. Agriculture & Co-operation Department
002. Education Department
003. Energy Department
004. Petro Chemicals Department
005. Finance Department
006. Food and Civil Supplies Department
007. Forests and Environment Department
008. Electricity Department
009. Health and Family Welfare Department
011. Industries and Mines Department
012. Information Department
013. Labour and Employment Department
014. Legal Department
015. Legislative and Parliamentary Affairs Department
016. Water Resources Department
017. Panchayats Department
018. Rural Housing Department
019. Ports Department
020. Transport Department
021. Revenue Department
022. Roads and Buildings Department
023. Rural Development
024. Science and Technology Department
025. Social Justice and Empowerment Department
026. Sports, Youth and Cultural Activities Department
027. Tribal Development Department
028. Urban Development Department
029. Women and Child Development Department
.
(CV.1) [ instruction to DC] if any citizen wants a change in this law-draft, he may submit an affidavit at Collector's office and Collector or his clerk will post the affidavit on the website of Prime Minister for a fee of Rs 20/- per page.
.
(CV.2) [ instruction to Talati or Patwari ] If any citizens want to register his opposition to this law-draft or any section or wants to register YES-NO to any affidavit submitted in above clause, and he comes to Talati's office with voter-ID and pays Rs 3 fee, Talati will enter YES/NO and give him a receipt. The YES-NO will be posted on the website of the Prime Minister.
.

Why did Jurors in USA opposed Slavery while Jurors in Greece supported slavery?
.
Greek states (back then Greece wasnt one country) started Jury System in 800 BC. And almost all Jurors supported slavery.
.
But in USA, in northern states, Jurors started opposing slavery. So much so, that when slave owners bribed Federal Senators to pass Fugitive Slave Law, the Jurors started giving verdict against the law. FSL = Fugitive Law stated that "if a slave runs away from state where slavery is legal (say Virgina) to a state where slavery is illegal (say New York) , then the policemen of destination state will arrest the slave and hand it back to the state policemen where the slave came from". The citizens in Northen States killed this law using two steps. The police chiefs who used to arrest the slaves and send them back without Jury Trial were threatened with expulsion !!! SO police chiefs started giving Jury Trial to arrested slave. In the trial, the Jurors used to say say "the arrested ,an is a legally free man, and so the law doesnt apply on him" !!!
.
So here, please note --- the slave owner was almost always a white man, and 12 Jurors too would be almost always all whitemen, and slave would a black man,. But all 12 Jurors used to side with blackman and give verdict against the white slave owner. Apparently, racisim didnt show up, or may be,m it was absent.
.
So question I put is --- why did Greek Jurors from 800 BC to 100 BC supported slavery , while US Jurors in northern states started opposing it from as early as 1810s. ?

MESSAGE TO MLA
---------------------------
.
Dear MLA,
.
If you got the link to this status via SMS , then it is an ORDER sent to you via your voter. The author of this status post has NOTHING to do with this order. The order is to print following law-draft in the State Govt Gazette. Pls order CM to print this law-draft in the State Govt Gazette. Please see link and filehashes in first lines of this status
.
======= start of the law-draft ====
.
PLD.0039.RtrGramSarpanch - Right to Recall Gram Sarpanch law draft
.
[ Comments :
.
(C.1.1) These comments are not part of the law-draft. They are only to provide information to the activists, voters and Jurors.
.
(C.1.2) This law-draft doesnt refer to any law-drafts.
.
(C.1.3) CM can print this draft, minus clause-10 in State Govt Gazette on his own. That wont need any law in Assembly or Parliament. The draft with clause-10 will need law in Assembly, but not in Parliament. The citizens may ask CM to print this law-draft using TcpState.
.
end of comments]
.
(1) [ general instructions ] The word citizen here means a registered voter in the Gram. The word 'may' means 'may or need not', and clearly means 'no binding'. The approvals given by a citizen will be displayed on a Govt website, along with his name and voterid. So approval giving is public and not confidential.
.
(2) [ procedure for Tahsildar aka Mamalatdar aka Block Officer ] Tahsildaar will accept a fee equal to deposit of Sarpanch and let a person register as alternate candidate for Sarpanch. The person must eligible to become Sarpanch on the date of filing his nomination. The person should give affidavits as necessary for Sarpanch. The approval counts of all candidates will become zero after an election or a poll as defined in later clauses.
.
(3) [ instruction for Talati aka Patwari (or his clerks) ] If a citizen comes in person to Talati's office, pays Rs 3 fee, and approves at most five persons for Sarpanch position, then the Talati would enter his approvals in the computer and would him a receipt with his voter-id#, date/time and the persons he approved. Talati would also allow voter to file approval for existing Sarpanch.
.
(4) [ instruction for Talati ] The Talati will put the approvals of the voters on website of CM with citizen's voter-ID number and names of the persons he approved.
.
(5) [ instruction for Talati ] If a the voter comes to cancel his Approvals, Talati will cancel one of more of his approvals without any fee.
.
(6) [ instruction for DC = District Collector ] If an alternate candidate gets approval of over 50% of ALL voters in that Village and it is also 5% higher than approvals obtained by existing sitting Sarpanch, then DC may hold a poll.
.
(7) [ instruction for DC ] In the alternate election, DC will allocate the first slot to the existing i.e. sitting Sarpanch. The existing Sarpanch will not be required to fill form. The other 8 candidates will be the 8 candidates who have highest approvals. And 10th row will be NOTA. There will not be any other candidate in the poll.
.
(8) [ instruction to DC ] The DC will declare an alternate Sarpanch as winner only if (a) he has obtained more votes than sitting Sarpanch AND (b) he has obtained over 51% of ALL registered voters OR he has obtained 5% more votes than votes obtained by the sitting Sarpanch in the election he had previously won.
.
(9) [ instruction to Sarpanch ] If an alternate candidate has won , then Sarpanch may or need not resign. If he resigns, then DC will hold a fresh Sarpanch election within 15 days.
.
(10) [ insruction to SEC = State Election Commissioner ] SEC will expel the existing Sarpanch and appoint the poll wimmer as new Sarpanch for the remainder of the term. And no election stated in clause-9 would be needed. This clause overwrites clause-9.
.
(11) [ instruction to Tahsildar , voters ] Tahsildar will erase all approvals after poll or election. The voters who want their approvals to continue may give their approvals again.
.
(12) [ instruction to CM ] With approval of 51% of ALL voters in the State, the CM may discontinue this RTR-Sarpanch procedure in a Village for 5 years.
.
(13) [ instruction to all, PM ] With approval of 51% of ALL voters in India, the PM may discontinue this RTR-Sarpanch procedure in a Village for 5 years.
.
===== end of law-draft =====

This year's UPSC IAS question --
.
A couple from India, both citizens of India, landed on airport in a foreign country. At immigration counter, husband and wife each stood in two DIFFERENT queues. And please note -- the country did NOT require men and women to stand in different queues.
.
i.e. both queues were open to both spouses.
.
And after sometime, one spouse left his queue and joined the other spouse in his queue.
.
1) WHY did they stand in different queues and not the same queue?
.
2) Name the couple
.
====
.
Added later --- correct answer came below in comments
