(1) हिन्दी भाषा के लिए हिन्दी लिपि और अंग्रेजी भाषा के लिए अंग्रेजी लिपि का प्रयोग करें। रोमन भाषा का प्रयोग नहीं करे ।
.
(2) दो पेराग्राफ के बीच '.' चिन्ह का प्रयोग करे ।
.
(3) लेखन में अनावश्यक चिन्हों जैसे '.......', '!!!!!!!', '//////', '####', '„„„„„„', '??????' आदि का तथा अनावश्यक 'केपिटल लेटर्स' का प्रयोग नहीं करें ।
.
(4) एक बार में एक ही कमेन्ट करे । यदि आपने दो कमेन्ट एक साथ किये है तो,
अ) पहले कमेन्ट को एडिट करें
ब) दुसरे कमेन्ट को कोपी करके पहले वाले कमेन्ट में पेस्ट करे
स) फिर दुसरे कमेन्ट को डिलीट कर दें ।
.
(5) कमेन्ट क्रमिक/सिलसिलेवार ढंग से करे, अक्रमिक रूप से नही ।
.
क्रमिक कमेन्ट क्या है ?
.
मान लीजिये कि
अ) आपने 'पहला' कमेन्ट किया है,
ब) मैंने 'दूसरा' कमेन्ट किया है,
स) आपने 'तीसरा' कमेन्ट किया है,
द) आपने चौथा कमेन्ट किया है ।
तो यहाँ पहला, दूसरा और तीसरा क्रमिक जबकि चौथा कमेन्ट अक्रमिक है । अक्रमिक कमेन्ट को बिन्दु 4 में बताये तरीके से संपादित कर पिछले कमेन्ट में जोड़ दें ।
.
(6) अपने प्रश्नों तथा टिप्पणियों को डिलीट नहीं करे । ताकि अन्य कार्यकर्ता भी इन्हें पढ़ सके ।
.
(7) कृपया मेरी फेसबुक प्रोफाइल पर नोट्स (Notes) सेक्शन में जाए तथा ' नागरिको को चाहिए कि वे अपने सांसद को SMS से आदेश भेजे' स्तम्भ को पढ़े । यह हमारे अभियान का केंद्र बिन्दु है । कृपया rahulmehta .com/301 .h. htm पर हमारे द्वारा प्रस्तावित क़ानून ड्राफ्ट्स का भी अध्ययन करें ।
.
(8) नोट्स सेक्शन में ही 'विशेष आलेख-01' शीर्षक से दर्ज स्तम्भ में राईट टू रिकाल ग्रुप द्वारा प्रस्तावित कुछ मुख्य कानूनी ड्राफ्ट्स की सूची तथा उनके लिंक देखे जा सकते है ।
.
(9) इस स्तम्भ का लिंक देखने के लिए नोट्स सेक्शन में जाकर 'आपसी संवाद स्तम्भ' या 'two person conversation thread' शीर्षक से रखे गए स्तम्भ को देखें । इस स्तम्भ का लिंक वहां दर्ज किया गया है ।
.
(10) हम देश में व्यवस्था परिवर्तन के लिए आवश्यक जूरी सिस्टम/राईट टू रिकाल कानूनों का मुक्त रूप से प्रचार करते है ताकि करोड़ो नागरिको की सहमति से इन कानूनों को गेजेट में प्रकाशित कराया जा सके । आप से अपेक्षा की जाती है कि प्रश्न/कमेंट्स पठनीय भाषा में रखें ।
.

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Sun Jul 24 2016 14:34:37 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

Right To Recall Against Corruption:

Before 1959, in our country there was a weak Jury system. In this system, the lower court, 9 citizens were randomly selected for deciding a case and these randomly selected citizens were called Jurors. These Jurors were changed after every case. Judge used to decide under which section case will be registered and Judge used to decide which evidences would be shown to the Jurors. The Jurors only could decide the case as guilty or not guilty UNDER THE REGISTERED SECTION and as per the evidences shown to them. Meaning, in the Jury system prevailing at that time, most of the powers were with the Judge and not with the Jurors.
.
This Jury system even though weak was quite beneficial to the society and to the country. Due to fear of being punished, that time, adulteration and hafta-giri was less at that time and bribes in courts were also less. But under a conspiracy, using excuse of Nanavati case, this system was abolished.
.
Nanavati was a naval officer. He was married to an English woman. He came to know that his wife had an affair with his close friend, Ahuja. Enraged, he killed Ahuja and surrendered himself to the police. This case was misrepresented by the paid media and in using excuse of this case, corrupt Nehru and corrupt Judges abolished this Jury system.
.
Those who use KM Nanavati case as an excuse for opposing Jury system should first answer one question -
Why did judges not charge on Nanavati Indian Penal Code section other than IPC 302 which had lesser punishment when Nanavati himself had confessed that he had killed Ahuja ?
.
Because if there were charges having less punishment, then Jury would have given Nanavati punishment under those charges while Ahuja family and nexused corrupt judges and corrupt media wanted maximum punishment for Nanavati.
.
Section 302 means killing with criminal intent that is murder and has maximum punishment of life sentence and in rare cases death by hanging.
.
Why did the Judge and lawyers not charge lesser IPC punishments ?
.
Because this was a highly politically influenced case. The family of Ahuja and Nanavati both had high political connections and had used them. Relatives of Ahuja had influenced the judges etc. to charge only max punishment and not lesser charges. Had these charges been there, jury could have ruled Guilty under those charges. And due to lack of sufficient, clear-cut evidence that Nanavati was guilty of pre-planned murder, they ruled "Not guilty under IPC 302"
.
This truth was not told by paid media that Nanavati was declared not guilty under IPC 302, So, in reality the paid media was paid not to tell reality that there was a conspiracy against Jury system. Why ?
.
Because the corrupt elite - corrupt Nehru and corrupt judges and other corrupt judges wanted to finish this system which was standing in the way of their corrupt ways. There seems to be no other reason because in the appeal there was no Jury and often after appeal influential people used to go scot free. In the end, due to influence of Nanavati on Nehru, he was freed by his sister, who was Governor of Maharashtra at that time. And the Judges never objected to this because the corrupt Judges were happy with abolishment of Jury system.
.
What is the proof that Jury was influenced by media ? Is there any testimony given by the Jury members to the press etc ? Was any study done comparing influence of paid media on Jury and on judges ?
.
Rather than doing a study of the systems of judge or the systems of jury - both existing and possible and improving the system, much worse system was brought. Citizens of other countries such as Hongkong improved this weak Jury system but in our country instead of improving this system, it was abolished and a much worse system was broughtYou can see and compare that even in those countries which have good jury systems, majority of judgments come quicker and are much more just. Please see more details in – tinyurl . com / JuryNichliCourt (remove spaces)
.
Also, consider this judgement to see how the judges themselves break laws - fb.com/notes/590527747632897
At least the jurors did not break any law and prosecution could appeal. There was no jury in higher court
.
All in all, if you compare apple to apple, the system proposed in chapter 21 of 3linelaw.wordpress.com is a hugely better system than today`s judicial system. It is judge system which should be abolished and a good jury system be brought.

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Sun Jul 24 2016 16:50:43 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

Activism, FBCPBA and "zero maintenance capital" i.e. work which can keep giving returns , even when no extra work is done
.
An activist can work for say 4 hours a week for a few months or years, and then may need to focus on career, business, legal problems, family problems etc. During this time, he may be almost zero hours a week.
.
So what should he do in 4 hours a week during those 1-2 years so that movement progresses due to his 2 years of work, even when he as now stopped doing work?
.
This is what is called as "zero maintenance capital" . Capital means something which can generate income, But in all capital, some hours have to be added. eg you have a shop which can earn income. But you need to spend say 8 hours to 10 hours a day on that shop. Now you can rent that shop or flat and generate some lesser income,. But to find tenant and maintain, you need to give say 100 hours a year. So time you have to give is less, income is less but some income keeps coming
.
Now in RTR, say one is distributing 1000 pamphlets a week. And say he stops doing that. Then now whatever work he did will not generate new activists once he stopped doing it.
.
So is there any low maintenance CAPITAL in activism for RTR?
.
IMO, FBCPBA = Facebook Cover Page Activism is"Very Low Maintenance Capital".
.
Say an activist works say 4 hours a week on FB, and likes / shares / copy-pastes drafts on his wall. and puts the links in a group called as "Law Drafts I support". Say he has put links to some 20-100 drafts. And say his cover page says "please click the link to see law-drafts I support".
.
Now say he stopped working. Then also, the cover page will keep working !!! Because every person who visits the coverpage will get some idea about the law-drafts he has supported.
.
IOW, FBCPBA = Facebook Cover Picture Based Activism is low maintenance capital !! Whatever you do, it will keep giving some returns for a long tine, even after you stop giving hours to activism

The unfixable (or whats the fix?) problem in private cellno / website to gather verifiable opinion - I can lower the cunt by hiding "support" SMSes. SOLUTION?
.
Say I am owner of a private cellno C and a private website W. And I have following system in place
.
(step-1) any voter V can register his voterid V on my website W by sending sms on my cellno C
.
(step-2) he can submit any proposal P and get id for the proposal. Say id for proposal P is P
.
(step-3) Then any voters V1, V2, ...V1000, ... V100000 can send sms supporting or opposing P, and I will display them with voterid on my website
.
(step-4) lets say there are no legal issues in displaying voterids and their SMSes.
.
Here is the problem of authencity,
,.
(1) say I put a proposal to bring MRCM in India,. Say proposal number is MRCM
.
(2) Say some 10 lakh registered voters send "I support MRCM" and some 1 lakh voters send SMS "I oppose MRCM".
.
(3) Now Jindal etc bribe me
.
(4). So I stop showing sms which say "I support MRCM" and show all sms "I oppose MRCM".
.
(5) So next, say 10 crore voters send sms "I support MRCM". and some 1 crore send sms "I oppose MRCM".
.
(6) I will show on my website that only 20 lakh have supported MRCM and 1 crore have opposed MRCM. IOW, after getting bribe from Jindal, I start deleting NEW SMSes (NOT OLD) SMSes that support MRCM. And I claim that "no, sms senders are lying, they never sent me SMS".
.
(7) Now worst activists can do is expose me, But Jindal will get me paidmedia who will say that I am 100% honest
.
IOW, PRIVATE cellno / website will not speak lies, but will hide truth AFTER the I = owner got bribe. And I will hide the new SMSes, not the old SMSes, which my bribe giver wants me to hide.
.
So private website does have GREAT demo value.
.
But IMO, it doesnt take verified count beyond a few 1000s. Because by the time verified count crosses a few thousand, bribe givers will come. And the bribe givers will either bribe the website owner OR create a new server and give it more publicity and push aside the non-corrupt website owner.
.
======
.
SOLUTION?
.
(1) Grand solution is TCP.. TCP is govt website and so it cant hide, If it tried to hide, then Jurors can narcotest the admins and find that they were hinding. After expelling a few admins, new admins will stop playing mischiefs. But what to do till TCP comes
.
(2) Ask sms-sending voter to send sms to MP and/or private website, and also send message on twitter., and in that message he can put @PmoIndia @HisMP, @PartyHeadofHisMP etc. Now if twitter deletes that tweet, at least one voter lost faith in twitter !! This is a big achievement.
.
(3) Ask sms-sending voter to send sms to MP and/or private website, and also write status post with link to the tweet. Now if FB or twitter deletes that status post or tweet, at least one voter lost faith in FB or twitter !!
.
(4) Eventual non-TCP solution is to write an APP, which when installed will create a digital signature for user and also ask for voterid, name etc. And that AAP will send public key and sha1 hash of that public key to everyone who is physically close the mobile and who is in "verified" list created by the phone owner. Now user can write "I support MRCM" and the AAP will use private key to create digital signature for this "I support MRCM" statement,. and it will send that statement to all physically close and in verified list. The message will be sent via wifi or via mobile-to-mobile socket and not via any server. This virtual network of connections can be used to get a good estimate of count. Accurate count is IMO NOT possible in private non-govt systems.

(2d) DcJurySys - District Court Jury System
(2e) HcJurySys - High Court Jury System
(2f) ScJurySys - Supreme Court Jury System
(2g) TcpDistrict - Decision of District Court Jury can be cancelled by TcpDistrict
(2h) TcpState - Decision of State High Court Jury can be cancelled by Assembly or TcpState
(2i) TcpIndia - Decision of Supreme Court Jury can be cancelled by Parialment or TcpIndia
.
Juries will consist of 15 to 1500 voters between age of 25 years to 55 years chosen from District / State or India depending on type of court and case
.

.

.
TcpDistrict < TcpState < TcpIndia
.
DcJurySys < TcpDistrict
.
HcJurySys < TcpState

.
All junior judges in District / Tahsil Courts are recruited by writtens exams and no interview

.
All SCjs are promoted from State High Courts by discretion of PM and Parliament
.
No lateral entry in judiciary except chief judges

.

And when SoGa became super-PM and defacto super-CM of several states of India, in year 2004, most states had less than 1% water connections metered. And % in year 2014 was same. And % of connections in Delhi which have water meter has not changes in past 18 months after Mahatma Arvind Gandhi became CM in feb-2015
.
As one worst example, Gujarat Housing Board in year 2013 made plans for 10 story middle class flats. And those flats dont have water meters !!! When water talk is on 10th floor, the first floor gets water at a huge speed, and if owners dont use flow controller, then within minutes, one tap will end up releasing 100 liter of water !!! In many 10 storey building I know,the water pumps' electricity bill is Rs 1000 per flat per month, even when some half the flats are empty !!! So imagine what would happen in those housing board middle class flats.
.
Now Gujarat Housing Board comes under NaMo, and their 10 story buildings dont have water meters !!
.
SoMoKe-andhbhagats claim that SoMoKe support universal water metering. But the above simple facts of past 15 years prove that SoMoKe are opposed to water meters.
.
So question is --- why are SoMoKe hostile to universal water metering? Pls give your reasons

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Sat Jul 30 2016 16:39:36 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

Details on "order your MP via sms/ twitter"
.
Full title --- citizens of India MUST send necessary orders to their PM, CM, MP, MLA, Mayor, Corporators, Sarpanchs, Panchayat Members over sms / twitter ; sms / tweets must have url and bittorrenthash of law-drafts you want
.
====
.
This FB status is to explain this "order your MP via SMS / twitter" campaign method that we activists at Right to recall party have proposed. This statuspost explains what we activists at Right to Recall Party are requesting MPs and 85 crore voters to do
.
===
.
List of chapters
.
(1) What do words MP and SMS mean?
(2) Request to PM, CMs, MPs, MLAs etc
(3) Request to 85 crore voters of India
(4) Request to all activists of India
.
=====
.
chap-1 : What do words MP and SMS mean here?
.
The word MP here includes PM, CMs, MPs, MLAs, Mayors, Corporators, Sarpanchs of District / Tahsil/ Gram, Panchayat Members of District / Tahsil / Gram, and word also includes Party's apex Leaders. eg consider statement "send order to MP via sms". So when issue is national subject, the word MP will mean PM / MP. And when issue is state subject, then word MP will mean CM / MLA. And when issue is city municipality subject, it will mean Mayor / Municipal Corporator. And if it is District / Tahsil / Gram subject, then it will mean Sarpanch and Panchayat Member of that District / Tahsil / Gram. And if MP , MLA etc has NOT put his official mobile number or twitterid on govt website, then sms can be sent to the party leader or MP / MLA of a nearby constituency of same party.
.
The word sms includes sms, twitter and facebook. eg "order your MP via SMS" means order the MP / PM or MLA / CM etc and also send a copy over twitter, and also mention it on facebook. If you dont know how to use FB, then it doesnt apply.

====
.
chap-2 : Request to all PM, CMs, MPs, MLAs, Sarpanchs, Mayors, Corporators, Panchayat Members etc
.
We request you to take following steps
.
(step-2.1) pls buy a new simcard and create a new twitterid.
.
Reason - This is necessary so that voters dont end up sending sms on your personal mobile or twitterid and dont end up disturbing you.
.
(step-2.2) pls put that new mobile number and twitterid on GoI website with sign (S) , meaning sms
,
Reason - This is necessary so that voters dont end up sending sms on your personal mobile or twitterid and dont end up disturbing you.
.
(step-2.3) if you dont want to count your voters, then please throw away the new simcard in a gutter. But if you wish to count voters, then please convert simcard into a virtual simcard or connect mobile phone containing simcard to PC. The PC can have following server.
.
Reason - It isnt possible for one or even 100 humans to process lakhs of sms. So sms that come should be fed to a PC and that PC will give summary reports to work on.
.
(step-2.4) If sms is "voterid = VOTERID" then server should register that voterid with that mobile number. To additionally verify, your staff send 4 digit code via postcard at the address of that voter as given in voterlist, and ensure that that code comes via that mobile via sms. Or you may ask voter to appear once at any of your ward offices or office of your political part and personally register his mobile number
.
(step-2.5) If sms is received from a mobile number whose voterid is not sent or known, then server should send send feedback sms as "pls send your voterid"
.
(step-2.6) The server should see if that sms has any url. If yes, then add that url in the list of urls received. If sms has word "#NO#" then see step-2.7. For each url, server should make a list of voterids and cellno which have sent at least one sms containing that url. And server should add the voterid , if known, to the list of voterids who have sent that specific url. And send feedback sms to sender as "your sms# is ___ . number of persons who have sent the url you sent , inclduing you are approximately = ____ ".
.
(step-2,7) If sms has url and has word "#NO" then server should remove the voterid and cellno from the list of voterid and cellno which have sent that url. And server should keep a log of date/time when that voterid was added / removed
.
(step-2.8) the server should see if that sms any hash such as filehash or bittorenthash. if yes, then server should add the hash in the list of hashes received. Then server should see if server has "#NO#" in sms. if "#NO#" is present, then server should take step-2.9. If hash is present and #NO# is not present, then server should add voterid, if known, and cellno in the list of voterids and cellno who have sent that hash. And send feedback sms to sender as "your sms# is ___ . number of persons who have sent hash you sent , inclduing you are approximately = ____ ".
.
(step-2.9) if sms has no hash and no link, then server should send feedback sms "pls send sms with link or hash . sms without url or hash cant be counted by this server".
.
(step-2.10) On website, server show links and hashes it has recieved . Staff should ensure that links dont have obscene text. In general. Also, you may make a policy that server will show only links to facebook or twitter or a govt website.
.
(step-2.11) Along with links and hashes, server must show count i.e. number of distinct cellno / voterids that have sent that hash / link
.
(step-2.12) on website, server may or need not show list of all phone numbers and all voterids. Server of PM or CM can surely show all sms, all phone numbers and all voterids after issuing a proper gazette notification. But if are not PM or CM, and you are MP< MLA, Corporator etc and you wish to disclose the mobile numbers or voterids of those who have sent sms to you, then first please check with a lawyer. Please ensure about possible legal issues. I , Rahul Chimanbhai Mehta, would not show voterids and cellno on my website, unless there is first a Gazette Notification that authorizes me to do so. You may decide on your own.
.
(step-2.13) But if server shows links / hashes you have received, and showing the voter counts or sms count will have NO legal problem, unless link as obscene text. So if policy is to show link of facebook status or twitter tweet or link to govt website, then chances that link have obscene status reduces.
.
(step-2.14) SPECIAL REQUEST TO PM \ CMS --- please ask DC's staff to allow voters to register their mobile number in voterlist. And if voter sends filehash or bittorrenthash via that registered mobile, then his sms should come on PM's website along with his voterid and name.
.
So we at Right to Recall Party request you to implement a server as above. The website smstoneta dot com has a demo version. You may look at that demo version
.
======
.
.
chap-3 : Request to 85 crore voters of India
.
Please see Satyarth Prakash , chap-6 , Rajdharm, written by Maharshi Dayanand Saraswati. the first or second page of chap-6 says "Raja i.e. king and ruling class should be Praajaadheen , or else such rulers using injustice and by using imposing fines using unjust laws, will rob the poeple ; the people will become weak and nation will get destroyed". And this shloka comes from Atharvaved.IOW, it is religious duty of every citizen of India to ensure that Raja i.e. ruler is prajaa-aadheen. Hence, it is your religious duty to send necessary orders to PM, CMs, MPs, MLAs, Sarpanchs, Mayors etc via sms / twitter..
.
The constitution says "we the people of Bharat ... " in its preamble,It doesnt say "we the MPs, or we the judges etc etc". It says "we the people", and word people means all us 85 crore voters of India. So it is us voters' constitutional duty as well as to send necessary orders to PM, CMs, MPs, MLAs, Sarpanchs, Mayors etc via sms / twitter..
.
Now before we and you 85 crore voters send orders, we should note that we commons can be only counted and not heard. i.e. MP can count us on 100s of issues, but he cant hear even 1% of us. Let me explain. Say each voter desires that MP should listen to him for 5 minutes.. Now MP has 17 lakh voters and even 25% voters speak for 5 minutes each, it is 17 lakh * 25/100 * 5 minutes = about 21 lakh minutes . And even if MP spends 10 hours a day listening, then also it would take him over 8 years to 9 years !!! IOW, it is materially impossible for an MP to "hear" your speech or read your letter or read your sms. And it is impossible for PM. Even corporator who has 1 lakh voters wont be able to hear voters below him.
..
So you can be heard. But you all can be counted. eg say 17 lakh voters want say 5000 different law-drafts . And one average voter wants say 100-200 law-drafts, 150 on an average. Then each voter can send 150 SMS with "codes" of the law-drafts he wants. That will be some 17 lakh * 150 = about 26 crore SMS. Now no human or even staff of 1000 humans can read 26 crore sms. But one PC can scan all 26 crore SMS and tell MP how many voters want law-draft-A, how many voters want law-draft-B and so on
.
IOW, MP can count us voters --- an MP cant hear us.
.
To those who want to be heard and not merely counted, sorry --- I have no solution for you. You must contact some other great leaders and great activists --- and India has 1000s of them !! They will promise you that each one of you will be heard, not merely counted
.
As per hearing the individual complaint --- that is something only a lower court Jury can do. I have discussed Jury System in other status posts
.
====
.
Now if you wish to be counted, then I request you to take following steps. If MP wants to count you, then these steps may enable you to ensure that you get counted. But if your MP doesnt want to count you, then please get an MP who agrees to count you.
.
steps we at Right to Recall Party request voters to take
.
(step-3.A) This step has to be taken once for each PM, CM, MP, MLA, Mayor, Corporator, Sarpanch of District / tahsil / Gram , Panchayat Member of District / Tahsil / Gram etc. This step is about getting their official cellno / twitterid
.
(step-3.A.1) pls get official, not personal , cellno of PM, MP, CM, MLA etc from govt website. Pls also get official twitterid of your PM, MP, CM, MLA etc from govt website. Pls send your voterid on the official cellno of PM, CM, MP, MLA, Mayor, Corporator etc. Also send your voterid to their twitterid.
.
Reason - It is unfair to send sms to personal number of MP. If all 17 lakh votes, or even 10% of them , send sms, then it will be huge mess for the MP. So sms should be sent on his official number which will be connected to PC if MP wants to count the voters, OR thrown in gutter if MP doesnt want to count voters
.
(step-3.A.2) If you cant get their official cellno / twitterid from govt websites, then pls send request via twit to their party leaders to put their official cellno
.
(step-3.A.3) If you still cant find official cellno / twitterid of you PM, CMs, MPs, MLAs, Mayors, Sarpanchs, Corporator, Panchayat Members etc then pls use cellno / twitterid of their party leaders. or MP / MLA of same political nearby constituency. Please also do write a post card asking MP etc to put official cellno / twitterid over govt websites. Pls also ask all your journalist friends to publicly request all PM, CMs, MPs, MLAs etc in their newspapers etc to request PM , CMs, MLAs etc that they all should mention their cellno and twitterid on govt websites.
.
(step-3.A.4) On facebook, pls create a group called as "Law Drafts I support". Please dont allow anyone to post status / comments in this group except yourself or your paid assistant.
.
Reason - when you share a post on wall, it will get pushed down by the new posts. So say there are 100 law-drafts you support and you have made some 2000-5000 posts. Then these 100 law-drafts posts will be scattered over 2000-5000 posts. But if a you have created a separate group called, and copy of law-drafts you support are in that group, then inside that group, there will only be posts related to law drafts you support and no other posts. So it will easy for you to reference. And it will be easy for others to see the law-drafts you support.
.
(step-3.A.5) ( optional ) Please create a cover picture which has only 2 lines of text repeated twice -- " pls click this cover picture to see the law-drafts I support" . In the text description to cover picture, pls put the link to the group "Law Drafts I Support". (eg As one sample, pls see my cover picture. You may download the same cover picture and upload it back as your cover picture. )
.
Reason - That way, the information on law-drafts you support will spread at a faster rate amongst your FB friends and profile visitor.
.
(step-3.A.6) ( optional ) Pls create a status post in the FB group "Law-drafts I Support". The heading should be "List of law-drafts I support and their links / hashes" . Please make it a pinned post.
.
Reason - If links / bittorrenthashes of all law-drafts you support are in one post, then information will spread at a faster rate and also you will be able to reference it easily
.
---
.
(step-3.B) We request you to take these steps once for every law-draft you support.
.
There are three levels --- depending on how much time you wish to allocate to publicize the law-drafts that you think can reduce the problems.
.
Level-1 supporter : if you wish to give minimal amount of time
----------------------------------------------------------
.
(step-3.B1.1) Pls ask activists around you to give law-drafts by which problems you are facing can reduce. If activists refuse to give law-drafts, then IMO, you need to change activists you support, follow and listen to. As an example, to see the law-drafts I propose, please click my cover page. And pls see text description, and pls click the link saying "law draft I support"
.
(step-3.B1.2) pls also ask activist to give internet link of facebook status where this law-draft is given. Please also ask him to provide sha1hash and bittorrenthash of the textfile or pdffile which has this law-draft. If activist doesnt have link or hash, then pls change activist. As an example, the above link will have links and hashes of the law-drafts I support.
.
(step-3.B1.3) pls send law-draft status link, law-draft file sha1hash and law-draft file bittorrenthash to PM / MP , CM / MLA, Mayor / Corporator etc as may be the case.
.
(step-3.B1.4) If the law-draft is central issue, then sms / tweet should be to PM / MP. If it the law-draft is State issue, then sms / tweet should be to CM / MLA. If law-draft is city municipality issue, then send sms / tweet to Mayor / Corporator. If law-draft is district / tahsil / gram issue, then pls send sms / tweet to Sarpanch and Panchayat Member of District / Tahsil / Gram.
.
(step-3.B1.5) If their cellno / twitterid is not known then pls use cellno / twitterid of Part's apex leaders or MP / MLA / Corporator of SAME party of nearby constituency.
.
(step-3.B1.6) In twitter, pls make sure that you have tagged PM, MP etc. In addition, you may tag a private activist or assistant who is doing record keeping on your behalf.
.
Reason : that way, that private activist can keep a log of all tweets you have sent, and put them in one status post on fb.

(step-3.B1.7) in addition, you may send that sms to a private activist's cellno if he is doing record keeping for you. eg you may send sms to smstoneta dot com. This private website will keep a log of all sms you have sent.
.
(step-3.B1.8) pls write a facebook status mentioning the sms, and the fact that you have sent the SMS to PM, MP etc. ; so that this law-draft gets publicity on FB.
.
(step-3.B1.9) pls keep a printout of all law-drafts you have supported with you in a file. Please keep date/time of when you sent the sms and when you tweeted to MP \ MLA ; so that you can show these law-drafts when you meet someone who doesnt have FB / twitter account and doesnt know how to use FB / twitter.
.
---------------
.
Level-2 : if you want to give 2 hours a week to publicize law-drafts
----------------------------------------------------
.
You need to take all steps above, and take additional step as follows
.
(step-3.B2.1) whenever you send FB status link , called as MainStatus, to your MP \ MLA of the law-draft you support, please take following steps
.
(a) add a comment "I support this law-draft" in the MainStatus.
(b) please like that MainStatus
(c) share that MainStatus on your wall
(d) share that MainStatus on wall of your "Law drafts I support" FB group, that you created in (step-3.A.4).
(e) put the link to twitter message you sent PM / MP as your fb status
(f) pls retweet the twitter message given in the first comment of the MainStatus
.
All these steps are so that information on law-drafts spreads at a faster rate, and the newcomer gets information on all law-drafts you have supported in one goi.
-----
.
Level-3 : if you want to give 8 hours a week to work to publicize the law-drafts
---------------------------
.
pls take steps (step-3.A.5) , (step-3.A.6)
.
(step-3.B3.2) In the text description of the cover picture, please put link to the group you have created in (step-3.A.4) .
.
(step-3.B3.3) Each time you like / share a law-draft, please copy-paste that law draft on your wall and also in the group law-draft I support.
.
(step-3.B3.4) In the group "Law-Drafts I Support" , please create a post which has one para for each law-draft you support. This post should be pinned post. That para should have following lines
.
(a) link to FB status post which has law-draft you support
(b) bittorrenthash of the file which has the law-draft
(c) link to the status on your wall , where you shared the MainStatus which has the law-draft
(d) link to the status on wall of your "Law Drafts I Support" group , where you shared the MainStatus which has the law-draft
(e) Link to the comment where you wrote "I support this law-draft" , in the MainStatus of that law-draft (see step-3.B2.1)
(f) Put all above link in the iSupport comment too
.
To see an example, pls click my profile picture Please click the link to "law-drafts I support" group, and pls see pinned post in that group, That group will have link to MainStatus of each law-draft I support and also link to MainStatus, shares, copies and iSupport links.
.
The above is what we call as FBCPBA = Facebook Cover Picture Based Activism. The details are at https://www.facebook.com/mehtarahulc/posts/10153344397466922 .
.
==============
.
chap-4 : Request to activists
------------------------------------
.
I broadly classify activists into two groups --- (a) those can only publicize law-drafts but not and (b) those who can develop / write law-drafts.
.
Anyone can learn how to write a law-draft to reduce a given problem. Some technical knowledge of the specific subhect may be needed. eg if you want to write a law draft about construction industry, then technical knowledge about construction business may be needed. Other then that, one also need some trainning in logic and maths. And to write law-draft, one has to first read a lot of law drafts , and see how other law drafts work or dont work !! .
.
So ability to write law-draft needs time, If one cant spare time for any reason, then he may have to confine to publicize law-drafts. and as time goes, he may be able to write law-drafts.
.
If you can publicize law-drafts, but not write law-drafts, then please do all steps mentioned above in Level-1 to Level-3. And in addition, please take following steps
.
steps-4.A are for activists who can work, but not create a new draft themselves,
.
steps-4.B are for activists who can TRANSLATE or write new law-drafts
.
----
.
(step-4.A.1) pls ask more and more friend of yours to steps steps mentioned in chap-3
.
(step-4.B.2) get more information about law-drafts, and publish on your FB status post profile and message them on WA
.
(step-4.B.3) also gather YT = YouTube videos on the law-drafts and circulate them on FB / WA / YT etc.
.
(step-4.B.4) pls distribute pamphlets on these law-drafts to activists who are working in these law-drafts
.
(step-4.B.5) pls make some site like smstoneta dot com to explain voters how PM's website will look like, once PM prints gazette notification by which all sms sent to PMO's cellno are displayed on that website along with voterid.
.
------
.
If you are an activist who can write law-drafts, then please take following steps
.
(step-4.B.1) pls write TRANSLATION or new law-draft in a textfile and or pdffile ; pls obtain the sha1hash of the file, and pls also obtain sha1hash of the text ; pls upload the file on bittorrent and get bitorrent hash. If it is translation, then please include hashes , but not links, of textfile or pdffile which has the law-draft
.
(step-4.B.2) pls upload the file on a facebook group ; in the file you upload, in the description section, please mention law-draft code, and sha1hash of file, sha1hash of the text, bittorrenthash, piecesize in obtaining torrent, and name of the file.
.
(step-4.B.3) please create a MainStatus for that law-draft. The MainStatus will have law-draft code, filehash, bittorrenthash, link to file (see step-4.B.2) , and also the whole text of the law-draft. The MainStatus should also have sms that voters need to send to MP.
.
(step-4.B.4) please put law-draft code, MainStatus link, bittorrenthash and filehash on twitter
.
(step-4.B.5) Pls publicize the law-draft, code, bittorrenthash, filehash etc using all possible means
.
For more, please follow my profile.
.

Pawan Jury BhilwaraRj

Sun Jul 31 2016 03:28:21 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

सलमान खान और काला हिरण --- जय है --- दरअसल हिरण ने आत्महत्या की थी ; सलमान खान निर्दोष है -- जय है, जय है, जय है !
.
फुटपाथ पर सो रहे लोगों पर भी गाड़ी सलमान ने नहीं चढ़ाई थी । उन्होंने भी सुसाइड कर लिया था -- जयहै !
.
एक बार फिर हाई कोर्ट जज ने घूस खाकर सलमान खान को रिहा कर दिया ; फिर से सोनिया-मोदी-केजरीवाल के अन्धभगत पूरी ताक़त से गला फाड़ कर चीख़ रहे है कि, "क्या किसी रिकालिस्ट के पास जज के घूस खाने का कोई सबूत है ?" और एक बार फिर से समाधान के लिए हमारा प्रस्ताव है कि, भारत में १) राइट टू रिकॉल जज, २) जयूरी सिस्टम और ३) सार्वजनिक रूप से नार्को टेस्ट लिए जाने का क़ानून लागू किया जाए । और एक बार फिर से सोनिया-मोदी-केजरीवाल के अंध भगत यह कहेंगे कि, "भारत के नागरिक ऊज्जड़ और मूर्ख है अतः उन्हें जजों को नौकरी से निकालने और मुक़दमों की सुनवाई कर फ़ैसला देने का अधिकार नही दिया जाना चाहिए । और जहाँ तक नार्को टेस्ट का प्रश्न है, ये टेस्ट मानवाधिकार के ख़िलाफ़ है" !! और इसी तरह और इसी तरह के बहाने ।
.
जब जज नशे में धुत्त गाड़ी चलाते हुए सलमान को इंसानों को कुचल देने के मामले में रिहा कर सकते है तो हिरण की तो फिर हैसियत ही क्या है । इसीलिए हमें हिरण को श्रद्धांजली देने के लिए और भी ऊँचे स्वर में जयकारे लगाने चाहिए -- "जय जय जय, जय है" !!!
.
बहरहाल हास्य, व्यंग्य को एक तरफ़ रख कर इस विषय के गम्भीर पक्ष पर ध्यान दीजिए ।
.
राइट टू रिकॉल ग्रुप के कार्यकर्ता पिछले १७ वर्षों से कह रहे है कि, "हमारी अदालतो के ९०% जज भ्रष्ट है, और इनकी मुश्के कसने के लिए हमें राइट टू रिकॉल जज एवं ज्यूरी प्रक्रियाओं की आवश्यकता है" । तथा जघन्य अपराधों में आरोपियों का सार्वजनिक रूप से नार्को टेस्ट लिया जाना चाहिए । मामलों की गंभीरता के अनुसार १५ से १५०० नागरिकों की जूरी बुलायी जानी चाहिए और सलमान खान जैसे रसूख़दार लोगों के मुक़दमों के निपटान के लिए पूरे देश से १५००-१५०० नागरिकों की दो ज्यूरी का गठन किया जाना चाहिए । इन नागरिकों का चयन मतदाता सूचियों से अक्रमत: विधि से किया जाए, और ये जूरी ही मुक़दमे का फ़ैसला दे । साथ ही नशे में गाड़ी चलाते हुए किए गए अपराधो में अनिवार्य रूप से आरोपी का सार्वजनिक नार्को टेस्ट लिया जाए ।
.
और पिछले १७ सालों से सोनिया-मोदी-केजरीवाल राइट टू रिकॉल, ज्यूरी सिस्टम, नार्को टेस्ट का पूरी शक्ति से विरोध कर रहे है । (१९९० में मोदी साहेब की भूमिका वाजपेयी निभा रहे थे और केजरीवाल की जगह हमारे पास सैंकड़ों एनजीओ थे ) सोनिया-मोदी-केजरीवाल के अंध भगतो और कोंग्रेस, बीजेपी, आप, संघ और भारत स्वाभिमान ट्रस्ट के सभी कार्यकर्ता भी सदा से राइट टू रिकॉल, ज्यूरी सिस्टम के विरोधी रहे है । पिछले ५००० सालों से ये लोग यही बहाना डोगरा रहे है कि ----
.
१) क्या तुम्हारे पास कोई सबूत है कि, भारत के जज रिश्वत ख़ोर है ?
.
२) चूँकि भारतीय नागरिक गवाँर और मूर्ख है अतः जनता के पास राइट टू रिकॉल, ज्यूरी प्रक्रियाएँ नही होनी चाहिए । साथ ही नार्को टेस्ट से आरोपियों के मनवाधिकारों का हनन होता है ।
.
३) न्यायपालिकाओ को दुरुस्त करने और जजों के भ्रष्टाचार को दूर करने के लिए सिर्फ़ सोनिया-मोदी-केजरीवाल जैसे सारे शक्तिमान नेताओं को चुन लेना काफ़ी है । इसके लिए राइट टू रिकॉल, ज्यूरी जैसी प्रक्रियाओं को लागू करने की कोई ज़रूरत नही । बताइए !!
.
इसी तर्ज़ पर २०१३ से २०१४ के बीच मोदी साहेब के अंध भगत और संघ के अंध सेवक पूरे देश को यह कह कर भ्रमित करते रहे कि, न्यायपालिका में सुधार के लिए कोई राइट टू रिकॉल, ज्यूरी सिस्टम क़ानूनो की ज़रूरत नही है । एक बार मोदी साहेब पीएम बन गए तो इस काम को वे सिर्फ़ १०० दिनो में निपटा देंगे । और अब ७०० दिन बीत जाने पर उनके पास १०० तरह के बहाने है !!! जैसे -- मोदी साहेब के पास राज्य सभा में बहुमत नही है, उनके पास दोनो सदनो में दो तिहाई बहुमत नही है आदि आदि ।
.
हालाँकि, एफ़डीआई और सिर्फ़ एफ़डीआई के कारण मोदी साहेब २०१९ में फिर से पीएम के लिए चुन लिए जाएँगे । उन्हें अग्रिम बधाई । लेकिन न्यायपालिका में संस्थागत भ्रष्टाचार कई गुना बढ़ जाएगा । इतने पर भी सोनिया-मोदी-केजरीवाल अगले १०० वर्षों तक सोनी सदनो में १००% बहुमत के साथ शासन करते रहेंगे किन्तु वे भारत की अदालतो का स्तर सुधारने के लिए आवश्यक कानूनो को कभी भी लागू नही करेंगे ।
.
समाधान ?
.
मैं देश के सभी कार्यकर्ताओं से इस तथ्य पर ध्यान देने का आग्रह करूँगा कि, सोनिया-मोदी-केजरीवाल की रूचि न्यायपालिका में व्याप्त भ्रष्टाचार और भाई भतीजावाद के समाधान में बिलकुल भी नही है । इसलिए न्यायपालिका में सुधार के इच्छुक कार्यकर्ताओं को सोनिया-मोदी-केजरीवाल के अन्ध्भक्तो और बीजेपी-कोंग्रेस-आम आदमी पार्टी के कार्यकर्ताओं से किनारा कर लेना चाहिए ।
.
कार्यकर्ताओं को चाहिए कि वे न्यायपालिका सुधार के लिए देश के नागरिकों को राइट टू रिकॉल, ज्यूरी सिस्टम, नार्को टेस्ट आदि क़ानूनी ड्राफ़्ट्स के बारे में सूचित करे । इन क़ानूनो के प्रस्तावित ड्राफ़्ट्स देखने के लिए कृपया मेरी फ़ेसबुक प्रोफ़ाईल के कवर पेज को क्लिक करके दिए गए लिंक में दर्ज ड्राफ़्ट्स का अध्ययन करें । यदि आप इन कानूनो का समर्थन करते है तो कृपया अपने सांसद को sms द्वारा आदेश भेजे कि, अमुक कानूनो को गेजेट में प्रकाशित किया जाए । सांसद को भेजे जाने वाले sms का नमूना उल्लेखित लिंक में दर्ज किया गया है । और एक बार फिर से मैं दोहराऊँगा कि यदि आपकी रूचि समाधान में है तो सोनिया-मोदी-केजरीवाल का समर्थन करने की जगह कृपया राइट टू रिकॉल पार्टी के कार्यकर्ताओं के साथ काम करें ।
.

.
फुटपाथ पर सो रहे लोगों पर भी गाड़ी सलमान ने नहीं चढ़ाई थी । उन्होंने भी सुसाइड कर लिया था -- जयहै !
.
एक बार फिर हाई कोर्ट जज ने घूस खाकर सलमान खान को रिहा कर दिया ; फिर से सोनिया-मोदी-केजरीवाल के अन्धभगत पूरी ताक़त से गला फाड़ कर चीख़ रहे है कि, "क्या किसी रिकालिस्ट के पास जज के घूस खाने का कोई सबूत है ?" और एक बार फिर से समाधान के लिए हमारा प्रस्ताव है कि, भारत में १) राइट टू रिकॉल जज, २) जयूरी सिस्टम और ३) सार्वजनिक रूप से नार्को टेस्ट लिए जाने का क़ानून लागू किया जाए । और एक बार फिर से सोनिया-मोदी-केजरीवाल के अंध भगत यह कहेंगे कि, "भारत के नागरिक ऊज्जड़ और मूर्ख है अतः उन्हें जजों को नौकरी से निकालने और मुक़दमों की सुनवाई कर फ़ैसला देने का अधिकार नही दिया जाना चाहिए । और जहाँ तक नार्को टेस्ट का प्रश्न है, ये टेस्ट मानवाधिकार के ख़िलाफ़ है" !! और इसी तरह और इसी तरह के बहाने ।
.
जब जज नशे में धुत्त गाड़ी चलाते हुए सलमान को इंसानों को कुचल देने के मामले में रिहा कर सकते है तो हिरण की तो फिर हैसियत ही क्या है । इसीलिए हमें हिरण को श्रद्धांजली देने के लिए और भी ऊँचे स्वर में जयकारे लगाने चाहिए -- "जय जय जय, जय है" !!!
.
बहरहाल हास्य, व्यंग्य को एक तरफ़ रख कर इस विषय के गम्भीर पक्ष पर ध्यान दीजिए ।
.
राइट टू रिकॉल ग्रुप के कार्यकर्ता पिछले १७ वर्षों से कह रहे है कि, "हमारी अदालतो के ९०% जज भ्रष्ट है, और इनकी मुश्के कसने के लिए हमें राइट टू रिकॉल जज एवं ज्यूरी प्रक्रियाओं की आवश्यकता है" । तथा जघन्य अपराधों में आरोपियों का सार्वजनिक रूप से नार्को टेस्ट लिया जाना चाहिए । मामलों की गंभीरता के अनुसार १५ से १५०० नागरिकों की जूरी बुलायी जानी चाहिए और सलमान खान जैसे रसूख़दार लोगों के मुक़दमों के निपटान के लिए पूरे देश से १५००-१५०० नागरिकों की दो ज्यूरी का गठन किया जाना चाहिए । इन नागरिकों का चयन मतदाता सूचियों से अक्रमत: विधि से किया जाए, और ये जूरी ही मुक़दमे का फ़ैसला दे । साथ ही नशे में गाड़ी चलाते हुए किए गए अपराधो में अनिवार्य रूप से आरोपी का सार्वजनिक नार्को टेस्ट लिया जाए ।
.
और पिछले १७ सालों से सोनिया-मोदी-केजरीवाल राइट टू रिकॉल, ज्यूरी सिस्टम, नार्को टेस्ट का पूरी शक्ति से विरोध कर रहे है । (१९९० में मोदी साहेब की भूमिका वाजपेयी निभा रहे थे और केजरीवाल की जगह हमारे पास सैंकड़ों एनजीओ थे ) सोनिया-मोदी-केजरीवाल के अंध भगतो और कोंग्रेस, बीजेपी, आप, संघ और भारत स्वाभिमान ट्रस्ट के सभी कार्यकर्ता भी सदा से राइट टू रिकॉल, ज्यूरी सिस्टम के विरोधी रहे है । पिछले ५००० सालों से ये लोग यही बहाना डोगरा रहे है कि ----
.
१) क्या तुम्हारे पास कोई सबूत है कि, भारत के जज रिश्वत ख़ोर है ?
.
२) चूँकि भारतीय नागरिक गवाँर और मूर्ख है अतः जनता के पास राइट टू रिकॉल, ज्यूरी प्रक्रियाएँ नही होनी चाहिए । साथ ही नार्को टेस्ट से आरोपियों के मनवाधिकारों का हनन होता है ।
.
३) न्यायपालिकाओ को दुरुस्त करने और जजों के भ्रष्टाचार को दूर करने के लिए सिर्फ़ सोनिया-मोदी-केजरीवाल जैसे सारे शक्तिमान नेताओं को चुन लेना काफ़ी है । इसके लिए राइट टू रिकॉल, ज्यूरी जैसी प्रक्रियाओं को लागू करने की कोई ज़रूरत नही । बताइए !!
.
इसी तर्ज़ पर २०१३ से २०१४ के बीच मोदी साहेब के अंध भगत और संघ के अंध सेवक पूरे देश को यह कह कर भ्रमित करते रहे कि, न्यायपालिका में सुधार के लिए कोई राइट टू रिकॉल, ज्यूरी सिस्टम क़ानूनो की ज़रूरत नही है । एक बार मोदी साहेब पीएम बन गए तो इस काम को वे सिर्फ़ १०० दिनो में निपटा देंगे । और अब ७०० दिन बीत जाने पर उनके पास १०० तरह के बहाने है !!! जैसे -- मोदी साहेब के पास राज्य सभा में बहुमत नही है, उनके पास दोनो सदनो में दो तिहाई बहुमत नही है आदि आदि ।
.
हालाँकि, एफ़डीआई और सिर्फ़ एफ़डीआई के कारण मोदी साहेब २०१९ में फिर से पीएम के लिए चुन लिए जाएँगे । उन्हें अग्रिम बधाई । लेकिन न्यायपालिका में संस्थागत भ्रष्टाचार कई गुना बढ़ जाएगा । इतने पर भी सोनिया-मोदी-केजरीवाल अगले १०० वर्षों तक सोनी सदनो में १००% बहुमत के साथ शासन करते रहेंगे किन्तु वे भारत की अदालतो का स्तर सुधारने के लिए आवश्यक कानूनो को कभी भी लागू नही करेंगे ।
.
समाधान ?
.
मैं देश के सभी कार्यकर्ताओं से इस तथ्य पर ध्यान देने का आग्रह करूँगा कि, सोनिया-मोदी-केजरीवाल की रूचि न्यायपालिका में व्याप्त भ्रष्टाचार और भाई भतीजावाद के समाधान में बिलकुल भी नही है । इसलिए न्यायपालिका में सुधार के इच्छुक कार्यकर्ताओं को सोनिया-मोदी-केजरीवाल के अन्ध्भक्तो और बीजेपी-कोंग्रेस-आम आदमी पार्टी के कार्यकर्ताओं से किनारा कर लेना चाहिए ।
.
कार्यकर्ताओं को चाहिए कि वे न्यायपालिका सुधार के लिए देश के नागरिकों को राइट टू रिकॉल, ज्यूरी सिस्टम, नार्को टेस्ट आदि क़ानूनी ड्राफ़्ट्स के बारे में सूचित करे । इन क़ानूनो के प्रस्तावित ड्राफ़्ट्स देखने के लिए कृपया मेरी फ़ेसबुक प्रोफ़ाईल के कवर पेज को क्लिक करके दिए गए लिंक में दर्ज ड्राफ़्ट्स का अध्ययन करें । यदि आप इन कानूनो का समर्थन करते है तो कृपया अपने सांसद को sms द्वारा आदेश भेजे कि, अमुक कानूनो को गेजेट में प्रकाशित किया जाए । सांसद को भेजे जाने वाले sms का नमूना उल्लेखित लिंक में दर्ज किया गया है । और एक बार फिर से मैं दोहराऊँगा कि यदि आपकी रूचि समाधान में है तो सोनिया-मोदी-केजरीवाल का समर्थन करने की जगह कृपया राइट टू रिकॉल पार्टी के कार्यकर्ताओं के साथ काम करें ।
.

Sun Jul 31 2016 13:58:02 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

Finally, all problems are reduced by engineers, doctors, scientists, technicians, labor and (real) investors. Then does world need activists?
.
Take any problem the commons or nation faces. The final solution comes only from engineer , scientists, doctors, technicians etc.
.
eg consider the problem, that women were kept suppressed in home. The fathers and brothers would restrict their daughters and sisters, and husbands used to keep their wives suppressed. The freedom came because scientists, engineers, doctors made blood test and later DNA tests to reduce paternity uncertainty to zero and also improved birth control to avoid unwanted pregnancy. With risk of unwanted pregnancy coming to zero, and with paternity uncertainity coming to zero, males lost reason to restrict their daughters, sisters or wives and women faced less risk. And so the 1000s of year old problem, that women had less freedom, reduced. Assorted feminists claim credit for their useless works. But at core, the solution came by science, engineering and medicine ONLY.
.
Next consider the problem of communication. Once, rich could afford telephones. And engineers and scientists worked hard, and made phone so dirt cheap that even starving can now afford a mobile phone !!
.
Next, consider the problem of heating and cold. Many used to die due to heat and cold. And now scientists, engineers etc made energy so cheap that heaters and fans and even acs may become affordable in times to come.
.
Likewise, consider problem of starvation. Some 100 years back, population was 2 billion and yet there was starvation. And now population is 7 billion and starvation has vanished. Again, it is because assorted scientists etc made improvements in irrigation, tractors to improvement in transportation.
.
So finally --- the questions comes before us all activists. If all problems eventually come down by scientists, engineers, doctors, technicians, labor and investors, --- then does world really need true activists?

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Sun Jul 31 2016 14:08:22 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

Can level engineering skills and total ability of society as a whole decease or can only increase?
.
Consider nation or whole world. Lets say that nation or world has certain level of engineering skills and achievement. eg say it can build road of certain quality and say it is capable of building say X km of roads every year
.
Then due to natural calamity or war, this sum total ability may go down down a few year. But can it go down in long run? Shoundt next generation be always better -- more skilled and more resourceful -- that previous generation? After all, even if next generation doesnt add any new innovation, then also it will be able to preserve the existing inventions / discoveries and maintain them.
.
So as such it appears that next generation should always be more skilled in engineering than previous one.
.
But not so
.
Romans could make roads in 100 BC and upto 300 AD, and later Europeans could NOT make such roads for next 1300 years !!! And likewise, Romans could make aqueducts to carry water, pipelines etc which Europeans could not make for next 1300 years.
.
Are such examples in other parts of world?
.
So what factors led to LOSS of skills and sum total of abilities? How can sum total of abilities and sum toatl of skill reduce? The reasons may be different for different society and time,. What could be reason for fall of skills in Europe? And other places where it may have happened?
